## Marine Personnel Regulations {#marine-personnel-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2007-115
:::

[CANADA SHIPPING ACT, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15)

Registration 2007-05-31

Marine Personnel Regulations

P.C. 2007-846 2007-05-31
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Transport, Infrastructure and Communities, pursuant to
paragraphs 35(1)(d), (e)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_523-e-fixed_hq_667){#fn_523-e-fixed_hq_667-ID0EICAA}
and (g)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_523-e-fixed_hq_667){#fn_523-e-fixed_hq_667-ID0EGCAA},
section 100 and subsection 120(1) of the [Canada Shipping Act,
2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_523-e-fixed_hq_668){#fn_523-e-fixed_hq_668-ID0EDCAA},
hereby makes the annexed [Marine Personnel
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-115).

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_523-e-fixed_hq_667-ID0EICAA){#fn_523-e-fixed_hq_667}S.C.
  2005, c. 29, s. 16(1)
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_523-e-fixed_hq_668-ID0EDCAA){#fn_523-e-fixed_hq_668}S.C.
  2001, c. 26
  :::
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-729302 .Part}

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Canada Shipping Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Administration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Administration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a Canadian vessel, the Minister;
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a foreign vessel, the
        administration of the state whose flag the vessel is entitled to
        fly. ([Administration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[air cushion vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[air cushion vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[ACV]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel designed so that the
      whole or a significant part of its weight can be supported,
      whether at rest or in motion, by a continuously generated cushion
      of air dependent for its effectiveness on the proximity of the
      vessel to the surface over which it
      operates. ([aéroglisseur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[applicant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[applicant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who applies to
      obtain, renew or replace a certificate or an endorsement issued
      under Part 1 or 2. ([candidat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[approved cadet training program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[approved cadet training program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      training program in either navigation or marine engineering,
      depending on the certificate or position sought, listed in the
      Department of Transport Standard TP 10655, Approved Training
      Courses. ([programme de formation approuvé de
      cadets]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[approved training course]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[approved training course]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a course
      that has been approved in accordance with section 114. ([cours de
      formation approuvé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[approved training program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[approved training program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a training
      program approved in accordance with section 114, given by a
      recognized institution and forming an integral part of the
      requirements for obtaining a certificate or an endorsement that,
      in cases provided for in these Regulations, may be credited to the
      applicant in lieu of a specified part of either the qualifying
      service or the examinations required for a certificate or an
      endorsement. ([programme de formation approuvé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ARPA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ARPA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an approved training course in
      simulated electronic navigation (SEN) entitled Automatic Radar
      Plotting Aids. ([APRA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[assistant engineer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[assistant engineer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, other
      than a rating, who is in training to become an
      engineer. ([officier mécanicien adjoint]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ballast control operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ballast control operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an
      MOU, has the same meaning as in IMO Resolution A.891(21),
      Recommendations on Training of Personnel on Mobile Offshore Units,
      or in any other resolution that replaces it. ([opérateur des
      commandes de ballasts]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[barge supervisor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[barge supervisor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an MOU,
      has the same meaning as in IMO Resolution A.891(21),
      Recommendations on Training of Personnel on Mobile Offshore Units,
      or in any other resolution that replaces it. ([superviseur de
      barge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[certificated person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[certificated person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of
      the manning of a survival craft, a person who holds

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Master or Mate certificate valid for the
        vessel on which the craft is carried;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a Proficiency in Survival Craft and Rescue
        Boats Other Than Fast Rescue Boats certificate or endorsement;
        or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a Restricted Proficiency in Survival Craft and
        Rescue Boats Other Than Fast Rescue Boats certificate that is
        valid for the vessel on which the person is employed. ([personne
        brevetée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[chemical tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[chemical tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      chapter I of the STCW Convention. ([bâtiment-citerne pour produits
      chimiques]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[chief engineer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[chief engineer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the engineer officer
      responsible for the mechanical propulsion and the operation and
      maintenance of the mechanical and electrical installations of a
      vessel. ([chef mécanicien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[chief mate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[chief mate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a vessel
      other than an MOU, the officer next in rank to the master and on
      whom the command and charge of the vessel falls in the event of
      the incapacity of the master. ([premier officier de
      pont]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[coasting trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[coasting trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Coasting Trade
      Act](/eng/acts/C-33.3). ([cabotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[complement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[complement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the master and the
      persons who constitute the crew of a
      vessel. ([effectif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[day work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[day work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means duties, other than
      watchkeeping, that are performed at sea on board a vessel
      involving the overhaul or maintenance of machinery in an engine
      room or boiler room, or on auxiliaries outside the engine room or
      boiler room. ([tâches quotidiennes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[deck officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deck officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[mate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, other than the
      master, a pilot or a rating, who holds a certificate that
      authorizes them to be the person in charge of the deck
      watch. ([officier de pont]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dynamically positioned]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dynamically positioned]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect
      of an MOU, that the MOU is held in position over a well wholly or
      partly by means of propulsion units. ([positionnement
      dynamique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[engineer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[engineer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person who may be in
      charge of the engineering watch, whether or not the person holds
      an Engineer certificate. ([officier mécanicien]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[engine-room rating]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[engine-room rating]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a rating who
      forms part of the engineering watch as the assistant to an
      engineer, but does not include a rating whose duties while on
      watch are of an unskilled nature. ([matelot de la salle des
      machines]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[examiner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[examiner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a marine safety inspector
      authorized under subsection 11(2) of the Act to administer
      examinations to any applicant and whose functions include auditing
      training programs and courses and making recommendations to the
      Minister with respect to the approval of training courses and
      programs. ([examinateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fishing vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fishing vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel used for
      commercially catching, harvesting or transporting fish or other
      living marine resources but does not include a vessel that is 24 m
      or more in length whose sole participation in those activities is
      in respect of the catch or harvest of another vessel or
      aquaculture facility. ([bâtiment de pêche]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[fitting out]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fitting out]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the time spent preparing
      a vessel for its operation during which time it is possible to
      substantially train an engineer, a rating or an assistant
      engineer. ([remise en fonction]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Great Lakes Basin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Great Lakes Basin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the waters of the
      Great Lakes, their connecting and tributary waters and the
      St. Lawrence River as far as the lower exit of the St. Lambert
      Lock at Montréal in the Province of Quebec. ([bassin des Grands
      Lacs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[high-speed craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[high-speed craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a craft certified
      by the Administration as meeting the requirements of the HSC
      Code. ([engin à grande vitesse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[HSC Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[HSC Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a high-speed craft referred to
        in sections 1.3.1 to 1.3.6 of the International Code of Safety
        for High Speed Craft, 2000, that Code; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of all other high-speed crafts, the
        International Code of Safety for High Speed Craft,
        1994. ([Recueil HSC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[IMO]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[IMO]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International Maritime
      Organization. ([OMI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[in charge of the deck watch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[in charge of the deck watch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in
      respect of a person, a person who has immediate charge of the
      navigation, manoeuvring, or operation of a vessel but does not
      include a pilot. ([chargé du quart à la
      passerelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inland voyage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inland voyage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 1 of the [Vessel Safety Certificates
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2021-135). ([voyage en eaux
      internes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inland waters of Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inland waters of Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in section 1 of the [Vessel Safety Certificates
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2021-135). ([eaux internes du
      Canada]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[international voyage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[international voyage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a voyage
      between a place in Canada and a place not in Canada or between
      places not in Canada. ([voyage international]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[laying up]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[laying up]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the time spent preparing a
      vessel for periods of inactivity, during which time it is possible
      to substantially train an engineer, a rating or an assistant
      engineer. ([mise au repos]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means registered length unless
      otherwise specified. ([longueur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[limited, contiguous waters voyage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[limited, contiguous waters voyage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      near coastal voyage, Class 1 limited to the waters contiguous to
      Canada, the United States (except Hawaii) and Saint Pierre and
      Miquelon. ([voyage limité en eaux contiguës]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[liquefied gas tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[liquefied gas tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning
      as in chapter I of the STCW Convention. ([bâtiment-citerne pour
      gaz liquéfié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[maintenance supervisor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[maintenance supervisor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an
      MOU, has the same meaning as in IMO Resolution A.891(21),
      Recommendations on Training of Personnel on Mobile Offshore Units,
      or in any other resolution that replaces it. ([chef de
      l'entretien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[marine industry association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marine industry association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
      grouping of owners of vessels operating under the Canadian flag
      that uses marine medical examiners for the medical examination of
      seafarers working on those vessels. ([association de propriétaires
      de l'industrie maritime]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[marine medical examiner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marine medical examiner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a physician
      who is designated by the Minister under section 268 to perform the
      examinations referred to in Division 8 of Part 2. ([médecin
      examinateur de la marine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[MED]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[MED]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[marine emergency
      duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means marine emergency duties taught
      at a recognized institution. ([FUM]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"}ou[fonctions d'urgence en mer]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
      Transport. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mobile offshore unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mobile offshore unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[MOU]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in IMO
      Resolution A.891(21), Recommendations on Training of Personnel on
      Mobile Offshore Units, or in any other resolution that replaces
      it. ([unité mobile au large]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
      [UML]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[motor vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[motor vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel whose
      propulsive power is derived from an internal combustion
      engine. ([bâtiment à moteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[MOU/self-elevating]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[MOU/self-elevating]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
      [[MOU/self-propelled]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and
      [[MOU/surface]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meanings,
      respectively, as in IMO Resolution A.891(21), Recommendations on
      Training of Personnel on Mobile Offshore Units, or in any other
      resolution that replaces
      it. ([UML/auto-élévatrice]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"},
      [UML/autopropulsée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} et
      [UML/surface]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[near coastal voyage, Class 1]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[near coastal voyage, Class 1]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      voyage

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is not a sheltered waters voyage or a near
        coastal voyage, Class 2;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is between places in Canada, the United
        States (except Hawaii), Saint Pierre and Miquelon, the West
        Indies, Mexico or Central America, or on the northeast coast of
        South America, including on any navigable waters connected with
        that coast; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} during which a vessel is always

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} north of latitude 6°N, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 200 nautical miles or less from shore or
          above the continental shelf. ([voyage à proximité du littoral,
          classe 1]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[near coastal voyage, Class 2]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[near coastal voyage, Class 2]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      voyage

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is not a sheltered waters voyage; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} during which a vessel is always

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 25 nautical miles or less from shore in
          waters contiguous to Canada, the United States (except Hawaii)
          or Saint Pierre and Miquelon, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 100 nautical miles or less from a place of
          refuge. ([voyage à proximité du littoral, classe
          2]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[offshore installation manager]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[offshore installation manager]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect
      of an MOU, has the same meaning as in IMO Resolution A.891(21),
      Recommendations on Training of Personnel on Mobile Offshore Units,
      or in any other resolution that replaces it. ([chef de
      l'installation au large]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[oil tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[oil tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      chapter I of the STCW Convention. ([pétrolier]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[passenger-carrying vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passenger-carrying vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel
      that carries one or more passengers. ([bâtiment transportant des
      passagers]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[passenger submersible craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passenger submersible craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel
      designed to be a passenger-carrying vessel and that can operate
      under water by relying on surface support, such as from a floating
      vessel or shore facility, for continued monitoring and for
      recharging

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} electrical power;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} high-pressure air; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} life-support systems. ([engin submersible
        transportant des passagers]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[place]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[place]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a voyage, means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a port; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a marine installation or vessel that is used
        for loading or unloading vessels. ([lieu]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[Pollution Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Pollution Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      International Convention for the Prevention of Pollution from
      Ships, 1973, signed at London on November 2, 1973, and the
      Protocol of 1978 relating to the Convention, signed at London on
      February 17, 1978, and any amendments, whenever made, to Protocol
      I, the Annexes or the Appendices to that Convention. ([Convention
      sur la pollution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[propulsive power]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[propulsive power]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the power in
      kilowatts that is specified in a vessel's certificate of registry
      or the total installed power in kilowatts that can be directed to
      propel the vessel, whichever is greater. ([puissance de
      propulsion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualifying service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualifying service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means service credited
      to an applicant in order for them to meet the experience
      requirements for a certificate or an endorsement issued under Part
      1. ([service admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[radio watch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[radio watch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the period during which
      a member of the complement is required to be at the ship station
      and in charge of communications. ([veille
      radioélectrique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[rated generator capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[rated generator capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      aggregate capacity obtained by adding together the individually
      rated capacities of all of the generators on a vessel. ([capacité
      nominale de production d'énergie électrique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[rating]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[rating]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a member of the complement of
      a vessel other than the master or an
      officer. ([matelot]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[recognized institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[recognized institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a training
      institution, designated by the Minister, that administers, in
      accordance with the established practices and requirements of the
      domestic and international marine industry, approved training
      courses and approved training programs for the purpose of giving
      applicants the training necessary to obtain a training
      certificate, a certificate of competency or an endorsement issued
      or required under these Regulations. ([établissement
      reconnu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[registered nurse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registered nurse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person registered
      or licensed as a nurse under the laws of a province. ([infirmier
      autorisée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sailing vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sailing vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel that has
      sufficient sail area for navigation under sails alone, whether or
      not fitted with mechanical means of propulsion. ([bâtiment à
      voile]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sea area A2]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sea area A2]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[sea area
      A3]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[sea area
      A4]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meanings, respectively, as
      in Chapter IV of SOLAS. ([zone océanique A2]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"}, [zone océanique A3]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}et[zone
      océanique A4]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seafarer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seafarer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is employed or
      is to be employed in any capacity on board a
      vessel. ([navigant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sea service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sea service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a period of qualifying
      service spent by a person employed on board a vessel and includes
      service while the vessel is in port, loading or unloading, at
      anchor, in refit or in dry-dock, if that service forms part of a
      voyage. ([service en mer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[second engineer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[second engineer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the engineer next to
      the chief engineer in the line of authority. ([officier mécanicien
      en second]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[SEN]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[SEN]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an approved training course in
      simulated electronic navigation. ([NES]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[sheltered waters voyage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sheltered waters voyage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in section 1 of the [Vessel Safety Certificates
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2021-135). ([voyage en eaux
      abritées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[SIM]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[SIM]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an examination on simulated
      electronic navigation administered by an
      examiner. ([SIM]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[SOLAS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[SOLAS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International Convention
      for the Safety of Life at Sea of 1974, and the Protocol of 1988
      relating to the Convention. ([SOLAS]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[stationary MOU]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[stationary MOU]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an MOU that is not
      underway, that is not maintaining position by means of thrusters
      or dynamic positioning or that does not keep the propulsion unit
      in standby mode. ([UML stationnaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[STCW Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[STCW Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Seafarers' Training,
      Certification and Watchkeeping Code adopted under the STCW
      Convention. ([Code STCW]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[STCW Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[STCW Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International
      Convention on Standards of Training, Certification and
      Watchkeeping for Seafarers, 1978. ([Convention
      STCW]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[steamship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[steamship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel whose propulsive
      power is derived from boilers and steam engines. ([bâtiment à
      vapeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[TP 2293]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[TP 2293]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the standard entitled The
      Examination and Certification of Seafarers, published by the
      Department of Transport. ([TP 2293]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[TP 4957]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[TP 4957]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the standard entitled Marine
      Emergency Duties Training Courses, published by the Department of
      Transport. ([TP 4957]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[TP 10937]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[TP 10937]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the standard entitled
      Mobile Offshore Unit Training Courses, published by the Department
      of Transport. ([TP 10937]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[training certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[training certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a document
      issued by a recognized institution evidencing the holder's
      successful completion of an approved training program or approved
      training course. ([certificat de formation]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[tug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel used exclusively in
      operations associated with towing another vessel or a floating
      object astern or alongside or in pushing another vessel or a
      floating object ahead. ([remorqueur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[unlimited voyage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unlimited voyage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a voyage that is
      not a sheltered waters voyage, a near coastal voyage, Class 2 or a
      near coastal voyage, Class 1. ([voyage illimité]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[vessel of open construction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vessel of open construction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel
      of which less than 50% of the length is covered full width, at or
      above the gunwale level, by decks or permanent
      enclosures. ([bâtiment non ponté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[watch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[watch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a vessel, that part of the
        complement that is required for the purpose of attending to the
        navigation, communications, machinery and security of the
        vessel, including, under the conditions of subsection 215(2),
        the master; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a member of the complement, the
        period during which the member is required to be on call or the
        physical presence of the member is required

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on the bridge or deck, in the case of the
        master, a mate or a deck rating, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the engine room, in the case of an engineer
        or engine-room rating. ([quart]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[wing-in-ground craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wing-in-ground craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning
      as in the International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at
      Sea, 1972. ([navion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in section A-VIII/2 of the STCW Code, every
    reference to the "International Regulations for Preventing
    Collisions at Sea, 1972" shall be read as a reference to the
    [Collision Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1416);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in section A-VIII/2 of the STCW Code, every
    reference to the Radio Regulations shall be read as a reference to
    the [Navigation Safety Regulations,
    2020](/eng/regulations/SOR-2020-216); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in IMO Resolution A.890(21), Principles of Safe
    Manning, or any other resolution that replaces it, every reference
    to "company" shall be read to mean "authorized representative" and
    every reference to an approved "Ship's Security Plan" shall be read
    to mean a "vessel security plan" as approved by the Minister under
    the [Marine Transportation Security
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2004-144) by the Minister.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of these Regulations, every reference
  to [[ro-ro passenger ship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in a document
  incorporated by reference in these Regulations means [[ro-ro vessel
  that is a passenger-carrying vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and every
  reference to [[air cushion vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means [[air
  cushion vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise indicated in these Regulations,
  any reference to a document incorporated by reference into these
  Regulations is a reference to the document as amended from time to
  time.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of interpreting any material
  incorporated by reference into these Regulations, "should" shall be
  read to mean "shall".

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the event of an inconsistency between a definition
  in any material incorporated by reference into these Regulations and
  any other definition in these Regulations, that other definition
  prevails to the extent of the inconsistency.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations and of all
  provisions of the Guidelines for Conducting Pre-sea and Periodic
  Medical Fitness Examinations for Seafarers, "medical standards" shall
  be read to mean the medical standards set out in those Guidelines and
  the additional standards provided in subsection 270(1).

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of interpreting the STCW Convention,
  [[sea service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
  subsection (1).

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of interpreting the STCW Convention
  and the STCW Code, [[Administration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
  meaning as in subsection (1).

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-729416 .Part}

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** In these Regulations,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Part 1 applies to applicants for a certificate of
  competency or an endorsement; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Part 2 applies in respect of Canadian vessels, other
  than pleasure craft, everywhere and in respect of foreign vessels in
  Canadian waters.

**\[3 to 99 reserved\]**

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Certification]{.HTitleText1} {#h-729421 .Part}

### [Certificates of Competency]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729422 .Subheading}

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may issue
the following certificates:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Master Mariner;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Master, Near Coastal;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} Master 3 000 Gross Tonnage, Near Coastal;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} Master 500 Gross Tonnage, Near Coastal;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} Master 3 000 Gross Tonnage, Domestic;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} Master 500 Gross Tonnage, Domestic;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} Master 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} Master, Limited;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} Chief Mate;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} Chief Mate, Near Coastal;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} Watchkeeping Mate;

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} Watchkeeping Mate, Near Coastal;

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} Chief Mate 500 Gross Tonnage, Domestic;

- [(n)]{.lawlabel} Chief Mate 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic;

- [(o)]{.lawlabel} Chief Mate, Limited;

- [(p)]{.lawlabel} Fishing Master, First Class;

- [(q)]{.lawlabel} Fishing Master, Second Class;

- [(r)]{.lawlabel} Fishing Master, Third Class;

- [(s)]{.lawlabel} Fishing Master, Fourth Class;

- [(t)]{.lawlabel} Certificate of Service as Master of a Fishing Vessel
  of Less Than 60 Gross Tonnage;

- [(u)]{.lawlabel} First-class Engineer, Motor Ship;

- [(v)]{.lawlabel} First-class Engineer, Steamship;

- [(w)]{.lawlabel} Second-class Engineer, Motor Ship;

- [(x)]{.lawlabel} Second-class Engineer, Steamship;

- [(y)]{.lawlabel} Third-class Engineer, Motor Ship;

- [(z)]{.lawlabel} Third-class Engineer, Steamship;

- [(aa)]{.lawlabel} Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship;

- [(bb)]{.lawlabel} Fourth-class Engineer, Steamship;

- [(cc)]{.lawlabel} Watchkeeping Engineer, Motor-driven Fishing Vessel;

- [(dd)]{.lawlabel} Small Vessel Machinery Operator;

- [(ee)]{.lawlabel} Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class I;

- [(ff)]{.lawlabel} Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class II;

- [(gg)]{.lawlabel} Proficiency in Fast Rescue Boats;

- [(hh)]{.lawlabel} Proficiency in Survival Craft and Rescue Boats Other
  Than Fast Rescue Boats;

- [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Restricted Proficiency in Survival Craft and Rescue
  Boats Other Than Fast Rescue Boats;

- [(jj)]{.lawlabel} Passenger Safety Management;

- [(kk)]{.lawlabel} Specialized Passenger Safety Management (Ro-Ro
  Vessels);

- [(ll)]{.lawlabel} Oil and Chemical Tanker Familiarization;

- [(mm)]{.lawlabel} Liquefied Gas Tanker Familiarization;

- [(nn)]{.lawlabel} Supervisor of an Oil Transfer Operation;

- [(oo)]{.lawlabel} Supervisor of an Oil Transfer Operation in Arctic
  Waters (North of 60° N);

- [(pp)]{.lawlabel} Supervisor of a Chemical Transfer Operation;

- [(qq)]{.lawlabel} Supervisor of a Liquefied Gas Transfer Operation;

- [(rr)]{.lawlabel} High-Speed Craft (HSC) Type Rating;

- [(ss)]{.lawlabel} Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Type Rating;

- [(tt)]{.lawlabel} Able Seafarer;

- [(uu)]{.lawlabel} Bridge Watch Rating;

- [(vv)]{.lawlabel} Engine-room Rating;

- [(ww)]{.lawlabel} Ship's Cook;

- [(xx)]{.lawlabel} Compass Adjuster;

- [(yy)]{.lawlabel} Offshore Installation Manager, MOU/surface;

- [(zz)]{.lawlabel} Offshore Installation Manager, MOU/self-elevating;

- [(aaa)]{.lawlabel} Barge Supervisor, MOU/surface;

- [(bbb)]{.lawlabel} Barge Supervisor, MOU/self-elevating;

- [(ccc)]{.lawlabel} Maintenance Supervisor, MOU/surface;

- [(ddd)]{.lawlabel} Maintenance Supervisor, MOU/self-elevating; and

- [(eee)]{.lawlabel} Ballast Control Operator.

### [Order of Priorities for Certificates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729481 .Subheading}

**[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** The certificates
referred to in paragraphs 100(u) to (ff) and (vv) are ranked as follows,
with a higher ranking certificate entitling its holder to all of the
rights and privileges of the holder of any lower ranking certificate:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the certificates referred to in paragraphs 100(u),
  (w), (y), (aa), (cc), (dd) and (vv) rank from highest to lowest;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the certificates referred to in paragraphs 100(v),
  (x), (z), (bb) and (vv) rank from highest to lowest; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the certificates referred to in paragraphs 100(ee)
  and (ff) rank from highest to lowest.

### [Endorsements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729486 .Subheading}

- **[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may issue the following endorsements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Oil and Chemical Tanker Familiarization;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Liquefied Gas Tanker Familiarization;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Specialized Oil Tanker Training;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} Specialized Chemical Tanker Training;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} Specialized Liquefied Gas Tanker Training;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} Passenger Safety Management;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} Specialized Passenger Safety Management (Ro-Ro
    Vessels);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} Proficiency in Fast Rescue Boats;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Proficiency in Survival Craft and Rescue Boats
    Other Than Fast Rescue Boats;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} Chief Engineer, Motor Ship;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} Chief Engineer, Steamship;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} Second Engineer, Motor Ship;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} Second Engineer, Steamship;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} Fore and Aft Sailing Vessel, Unlimited;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} Square Rig Sailing Vessel, Unlimited;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} Fore and Aft Sailing Vessel, Seasonal (April 15 to
    November 1);

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} Square Rig Sailing Vessel, Seasonal (April 15 to
    November 1);

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} Limited, Contiguous Waters Voyage, in respect of
    the following certificates of competency:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Master 500 Gross Tonnage, Domestic,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Master 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} Chief Mate 500 Gross Tonnage, Domestic, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} Chief Mate 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic; and

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} Passenger Submersible Craft.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall issue an endorsement only in
  relation to a certificate of competency that the Minister has issued.

### [Period of Validity of Training Courses for a New Certificate or Endorsement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729513 .Subheading}

- **[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (4),
  a certificate or an endorsement in respect of which successful
  completion of a training course in any of the following
  subject-matters is required may only be issued if the course was
  successfully completed within the 5 years before the application date
  for issuance of the certificate or endorsement:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} marine emergency duties;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} simulated electronic navigation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} propulsive plant simulation.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an applicant has successfully completed a course
  referred to in subsection (1) more than 5 years before the application
  date for issuance of the certificate or endorsement, then the
  following course equivalency shall be accepted:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a training testimonial indicating that they have
    successfully completed a refresher training course in that subject
    within the 5 years before the application date for issuance of the
    certificate or endorsement;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 12 months of service on board one or more
    vessels, performing deck or engine-room duties, as applicable,
    within the 5 years before the application date for issuance of the
    certificate or endorsement, supported, in the case of a training
    course in marine emergency duties, by a testimonial attesting to
    competent participation in emergency drills; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at least 3 months of service on board one or more
    vessels, performing deck or engine-room duties, as applicable, in
    the 12-month period before the application date for issuance of the
    certificate or endorsement, supported, in the case of a training
    course in marine emergency duties, by a testimonial attesting to
    competent participation in emergency drills.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The successful completion of a course that was taken
  for the purpose of obtaining a certificate of competency or an
  endorsement remains valid for life once that certificate or
  endorsement has been obtained, except in the case of the following
  courses:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a course referred to in subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a first aid course;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a course with respect to tankers; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a course with respect to passenger-carrying
    vessels.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A course in marine emergency duties listed in any of
  the tables to sections 123 to 182 and Schedule 1 to this Part setting
  out the requirements applicable to the various certificates of
  competency and endorsements may be substituted by a course identified
  in TP 4957 as being equivalent if that course was successfully
  completed before this section comes into force.

### [Exchanges or Renewals of Certificates and Endorsements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729529 .Subheading}

**[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** Except in the cases set
out in section 105, the holder of a Master, a Mate or an Engineer
certificate that was issued before the day on which this section comes
into force, that has not been renewed under these Regulations and that
is set out in column 1 of Schedule 1 to this Part may obtain, in order
to continue to have a certificate that is valid for use at sea and at
the latest on the date of the expiry of the certificate, on application,
an exchange of that certificate for one of the corresponding
certificates set out in column 3 having, where applicable, the
endorsement and limitation set out in column 4 of that item, and for
that purpose the holder shall meet the requirements set out in column 2
of that item or paragraph, if any, and the requirements of section 106
or 107, as the case may be.

**[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** An applicant who holds
any one of the certificates referred to in column 1 of paragraphs 10(e),
22(b), 24(e), 25(b), 26(b) and 27(b), items 28 to 33, paragraphs 38(a)
or (b), 39(a) or (b) and items 40 and 41 of Schedule 1 to this Part may,
in order to continue to have a certificate that is valid for use at sea,
obtain, on application, a renewal of the certificate, subject to any
limitations mentioned on the certificate after its renewal or that are
set out in column 1 of the corresponding item or paragraph, if the
applicant meets the requirements set out in column 2, if any, and the
requirements of section 106 or 107, as the case may be.

- **[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for the renewal of a certificate referred to in any of
  paragraphs 100(a) to (t), (yy) to (bbb) and (eee) or that has been
  renewed under section 105 shall meet one of the following
  requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquire the service set out in subsection (2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide the examiner with a training testimonial
    indicating that they have successfully completed, within the 5 years
    before the application date for renewal, a refresher training course
    in marine emergency duties at the level applicable to the
    certificate sought to be renewed, and have passed, within that same
    period,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a certificate referred to in
      paragraph 100(h) or (o), a practical and oral examination on
      general seamanship applicable to the vessel in respect of which
      renewal of the certificate is sought, in accordance with the
      vessel's gross tonnage, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other certificate, a written
      examination on navigation safety and an oral examination on
      general seamanship; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} provide the examiner with training testimonials
    indicating that they have successfully completed, within the 5 years
    before the application date for renewal, the following courses at
    the level applicable to the certificate sought:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ship management, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} refresher training course in marine emergency
      duties.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant who chooses to meet the requirements of
  paragraph (1)(a) shall acquire the following service:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (3), at least 12 months of
    sea service on board one or more vessels as a master or mate in
    charge of the deck watch or on board one or more MOUs as an offshore
    installation manager, a barge supervisor or a ballast control
    operator acquired within the 5 years before the application date for
    renewal of the certificate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (3), at least 3 months of sea
    service on board one or more vessels as a master, mate or
    supernumerary mate performing watchkeeping duties, or on board one
    or more MOUs as an offshore installation manager, a barge supervisor
    or a ballast control operator acquired within the 12 months
    immediately before the application date for renewal of the
    certificate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at least 24 months of service, within the 5 years
    before the application date for renewal of the certificate,
    performing duties in any of the following marine-related positions:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a shore captain, marine superintendent or
      operations manager while employed by the authorized representative
      of a vessel,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a licensed ship's pilot assigned to pilotage
      duties or an operations manager employed by a pilotage authority,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a marine surveyor or marine inspector
      performing duties relating to the survey or inspection of vessels
      or vessels' equipment or cargoes,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a harbour master, dock master, berth master,
      deputy harbour master, deputy dock master or deputy berth master,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a watchkeeper or supervisor of port operations,
      vessel traffic services or a search and rescue centre,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a hydrographic surveyor,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} an instructor or training officer in nautical
      subjects, employed at a recognized institution or by an authorized
      representative,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} an examiner of masters and mates,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} a casualty investigator engaged in investigating
      marine casualties,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} a person engaged in marine emergency response
      planning or vessel's operations, or

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} an officer or representative of a trade union,
      as defined in the [Canada Labour Code](/eng/acts/L-2), whose
      duties include

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} involvement in marine casualty investigations,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} liaising with or providing assistance to
        government bodies such as the Department of Transport and the
        Transportation Safety Board of Canada, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} participating in the development of policy with
        respect to the training of ship's officers; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an equivalent prorated combination of the
    requirements set out in paragraphs (a) to (c).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a certificate referred to in paragraph
  100(h) or (o), the sea service must be acquired on board the vessel or
  vessels for which it is valid and in the area of operation specified
  on the certificate.

<!-- -->

- **[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for the renewal of an Engineer certificate referred to in
  any of paragraphs 100(u) to (dd), (ccc) and (ddd) shall meet one of
  the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquire the service set out in subsection (3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide the examiner with a training testimonial
    indicating that they have successfully completed, within the 5 years
    before the application date for renewal, a refresher training course
    in marine emergency duties at the level applicable to the
    certificate sought, pass within that same period a written or oral
    examination on general engineering knowledge and at any time, have
    successfully completed a course in propulsive plant simulation at
    the level applicable to the certificate sought; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} provide the examiner with a training testimonial
    indicating that they have successfully completed, within the 5 years
    before the application date for renewal, a refresher training course
    in marine emergency duties at the level applicable to the
    certificate sought, have successfully completed at any time a course
    in propulsive plant simulation at the level applicable to the
    certificate sought and, in addition, have successfully completed
    within the 5 years before the application date for renewal, a
    training course in

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} automation, control and instrumentation, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} marine power systems.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant referred to in subsection (1), other
  than an applicant for the renewal of the certificate referred to in
  paragraph 100(dd), who has acquired at least three years of service on
  board a specific vessel or a sister vessel, within the 5 years before
  the application date for renewal of the certificate, is not required
  to provide a training certificate in propulsive plant simulation, but,
  without providing that training certificate, the applicant's renewed
  certificate is restricted to that vessel or sister vessel and the
  areas in which that vessel or sister vessel operated during that
  period of service.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant who chooses to meet the requirements of
  paragraph (1)(a) shall acquire the following service:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 12 months of sea service as an engineer
    officer on one or more vessels within the 5 years before the
    application date for renewal of the certificate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 3 months of sea service as an engineer
    officer on one or more vessels within the 12 months before the
    application date for renewal of the certificate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at least 24 months of service, within the 5 years
    before the application date for renewal of the certificate,
    performing functions in any of the following marine-related
    positions:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} marine engineer superintendent or operating
      engineer manager, while employed by an authorized representative,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} marine surveyor or marine inspector performing
      duties relating to the survey or inspection of vessels or vessel's
      machinery, equipment or cargoes,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} instructor or training officer in marine
      engineering subjects, while employed at a recognized institution
      or by an authorized representative,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} examiner of engineers,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} casualty investigator engaged in investigating
      marine casualties,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} person engaged in marine emergency response
      planning or vessel's operations, or

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} an officer or representative of a trade union
      as defined in the [Canada Labour Code](/eng/acts/L-2) whose duties
      include

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} involvement in marine casualty investigations,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} liaising with or providing assistance to
        government bodies such as the Department of Transport and the
        Transportation Safety Board of Canada, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} participating in the development of policy with
        respect to the training of ship's officers; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an equivalent prorated combination of the
    requirements set out in paragraphs (a) to (c).

**[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for the
renewal of a certificate referred to in any of paragraphs 100(nn) to
(qq) issued under these Regulations or an equivalent supervisor of
transfer operations certificate issued under the [Marine Certification
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-97-391) shall provide the examiner
with a training certificate in marine emergency duties with respect to
STCW basic safety and shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} have acquired at least 3 months of service within the
  5 years before the application date for renewal of the certificate,
  which service shall include duties relating to oil, chemical or
  liquefied gas transfer operations relating to the certificate sought
  and, if the applicant is the holder of a Supervisor of an Oil Transfer
  Operation in Arctic Waters (North of 60° N) certificate, provide the
  examiner with a testimonial of the applicant's completion, within the
  5 years before that date, of at least three transfers in waters north
  of 60° N; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide the examiner with a training certificate
  obtained within the 5 years before the application date for renewal of
  the certificate, with respect to a course in supervision of an oil
  transfer operation, supervision of a chemical transfer operation or
  specialized liquefied gas tanker safety relating to the certificate
  sought.

<!-- -->

- **[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for renewal of the certificate referred to in paragraph
  100(jj) or the endorsement referred to in paragraph 102(f) shall
  provide the examiner with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a training testimonial indicating that, within the
    5 years before the application date for renewal, they have
    successfully completed a refresher training course in passenger
    safety management; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} testimonial of at least 3 months of service, within
    the 5 years before the application date for renewal, on one or more
    passenger-carrying vessels, attesting to competent participation in
    emergency drills.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for renewal of the certificate referred
  to in paragraph 100(kk) or the endorsement referred to in paragraph
  102(g) shall provide the examiner with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a training testimonial indicating that, within the
    5 years before the application date for renewal, they have
    successfully completed a refresher training course in specialized
    passenger safety management (ro-ro vessels); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} testimonial of at least 3 months of service, within
    the 5 years before the application date for renewal, on one or more
    ro-ro vessels that are passenger-carrying vessels, attesting to
    competent participation in emergency drills.

### [Application for Examination and Eligibility]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729594 .Subheading}

- **[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise indicated in these
  Regulations, an applicant for a certificate of competency or an
  endorsement issued under these Regulations shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be at least 18 years of age on the day on which
    they receive the certificate or endorsement; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have started to acquire the sea service required to
    obtain the certificate or endorsement after having reached 16 years
    of age or, in the case of service acquired on a fishing vessel in
    respect of an application for a fishing certificate, 15 years of
    age.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant shall provide the following to the
  examiner, at least two weeks before the first of the examinations
  referred to in these Regulations that the applicant seeks to take:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a signed application containing the information and
    declarations set out in Schedule 2 to this Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's birth certificate or equivalent;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all of the discharge books and any certificates of
    discharge in respect of the qualifying service required for the
    certificate or endorsement sought;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any testimonials, containing the required
    signatures and at least the information set out in Schedule 4 or 5
    to this Part, as the case may be, in respect of the sea service
    required for the certificate or endorsement sought; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any pertinent certificate held by the applicant,
    including certificates issued by an Administration, other than the
    Minister, recognized by the IMO as being compliant with the STCW
    Convention.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An application made under these Regulations for an
  examination in respect of the issuance, exchange or renewal of a
  certificate of competency or an endorsement remains valid for a period
  of 1 year after the day on which the examiner receives the
  application.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} An applicant who, as part of an approved training
  program, is required to maintain and complete a training record book
  while on board a vessel shall, in order to have that training
  recognized, present to the examiner the completed book, evaluated as
  being satisfactory by the recognized institution responsible for that
  program.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} An applicant, in order to be eligible for an
  examination, shall pay the applicable fees set out in column 2 of the
  table to subsection 121(1) each time the applicant takes the
  examination.

**[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** Before a certificate of
competency or an endorsement is issued to an applicant, the applicant
shall provide the examiner with

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} proof that the applicant is a qualified person; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} except if the applicant is applying for a certificate
  of competency referred to in section 131 that is valid in respect of a
  vessel that is not a passenger-carrying vessel or in section 138, 143,
  151 or 174, either a medical certificate or a provisional medical
  certificate that is in effect and that was issued in respect of the
  applicant in accordance with Division 8 of Part 2 attesting to the
  applicant's fitness to

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} perform the duties to which the certificate of
    competency or endorsement relates, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} make the voyages that the certificate of
    competency or endorsement authorizes.

### [Examinations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729613 .Subheading}

- **[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  examiner shall ensure that an applicant follows the examination
  procedures established by TP 2293 to ensure that examinations are
  conducted properly and that standards of objective evaluation,
  fairness, merit and equity are upheld.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant who does not follow the procedures
  referred to in subsection (1) automatically fails the examination.

<!-- -->

- **[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  passing grade obtained in any of the following examinations remains
  valid for 5 years after the date of the examination:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an examination taken for the purpose of obtaining a
    Master, a Mate, an Able Seafarer or a Bridge Watch Rating
    certificate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one of the following engineering examinations when
    taken for the purpose of obtaining an Engineer certificate:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} general engineering knowledge,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} engineering knowledge of motor vessels, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} engineering knowledge of steamships.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A passing grade obtained in an engineering
  examination taken for the purpose of obtaining an Engineer
  certificate, other than an examination referred to in paragraph
  (1)(b), remains valid for life.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A passing grade in an examination remains valid for
  life after the certificate of competency or endorsement, in respect of
  which the examination was taken, is obtained or renewed.

### [Approved Training]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729626 .Subheading}

**[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114 .sectionLabel}** The Minister, on the
recommendation of an examiner, shall approve a training program or a
training course as an approved training program or approved training
course, as the case may be, if it meets the standards respecting the
training and assessment of seafarers that are applicable to the training
to be provided and assessment to be performed, as set out in the Quality
Management Manual --- Marine Personnel Standards and Pilotage, in the
chapter entitled Approval of Marine Training Courses and Programs, dated
July 30, 2001, as amended in June 2007, established in accordance with
Regulation I/8 of the STCW Convention and published by the Department of
Transport.

### [Computation of Qualifying Service]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729628 .Subheading}

- **[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of calculating sea service, 8 hours of service equals one
  day of sea service.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Service for watches of other than 8 hours in a
  calendar day shall be prorated to a maximum of 12 hours for that day
  based on a 12-hour watch equalling one and one-half days of sea
  service except for applicants registered in an approved cadet training
  program whose sea service shall be calculated on the basis of any
  amount of service in one calendar day equalling one day of service.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In calculating the number of months of qualifying
  service, the total number of days of credited sea service shall be
  divided by 30.

**[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
specified in these Regulations, service on board an MOU or any other
vessel, including a vessel engaged in commercial activities other than
the carriage of passengers or the handling of cargo, shall be assessed
as qualifying service in accordance with Chapter 3 of TP 2293.

**[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117 .sectionLabel}** Sea service acquired as
a junior officer to a mate in charge of the deck watch shall be credited
at the ratio of two thirds of a day of sea service for every day of
actual sea service, taking into consideration the limitations set out in
section 115, up to a maximum of 9 months, for the purpose of obtaining
any Master or Mate certificate.

**[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** If an applicant deserts
a vessel after making an agreement with the crew undertaking to serve on
that vessel, any sea service performed by the applicant on that vessel
before the applicant's desertion is not recognized in the computation of
sea service for the purpose of obtaining any certificate or endorsement.

### [Direct Examination]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729636 .Subheading}

- **[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  qualified person who wishes to obtain a certificate issued under these
  Regulations and who is the holder of one of the following documents
  may apply for an examiner to assess their qualifications against the
  requirements of these Regulations for the purpose of obtaining a
  certificate of an equivalent or lower rank:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a certificate issued under the STCW Convention,
    valid for use at sea without restrictions as to tonnage or class of
    voyage, issued by an Administration recognized by the IMO as being
    STCW compliant;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a certificate of service issued by the Minister;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a qualification issued by the Department of
    National Defence, including but not limited to deck and engineering
    qualifications; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian Coast Guard Watchkeeping or Command
    certificate.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The examiner shall assess the applicant's
  qualifications against the requirements of these Regulations by
  adhering to the evaluation procedures and criteria set out in TP 2293
  that are specified as being applicable to the applicant's situation.

### [Other Conditions Applicable to Certificates and Endorsements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729644 .Subheading}

- **[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  certificate of competency or an endorsement required under these
  Regulations shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if it is issued after the day on which this section
    comes into force, be issued under this Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if it was issued on or after July 30, 1997 but
    before the day on which this section comes into force, have been
    issued under the [Marine Certification
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-97-391); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if it was issued before July 30, 1997, have been
    issued under one of the following Regulations:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Certification of Able Seamen Regulations,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Certification of Lifeboat Men Regulations,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the Certification of Ships' Cooks Regulations,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the Marine Engineer Examination Regulations, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the Masters and Mates Examination Regulations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to meeting the requirements of subsection
  (1), a certificate of competency shall be endorsed as meeting the
  requirements of the STCW Convention, except if the certificate is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} valid only on a fishing vessel, a high-speed craft,
    a passenger submersible craft, an air cushion vessel or a mobile
    offshore unit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} valid only for a limited, contiguous waters voyage,
    a near coastal voyage, Class 2 or a sheltered waters voyage;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a master, inland waters certificate (CIW) issued
    before September 1, 1977;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a master, ship of not more than 350 tons, gross
    tonnage, or tug, home trade voyage certificate (CHT 350) issued
    before September 1, 1977;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a master, ship of not more than 350 tons, gross
    tonnage, or tug, inland waters voyage certificate (CIW 350) issued
    before September 1, 1977;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a certificate of service as master of a steamship
    of not more than 350 tons, gross tonnage;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a first mate, inland waters certificate (1MIW)
    issued on or after March 23, 1966 but before September 1, 1976;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a second mate, inland waters certificate (2MIW)
    issued on or after March 23, 1966 but before September 1, 1975;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a certificate of service as master of a ship of not
    more than 1 600 tons, gross tonnage;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a compass adjuster certificate;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a certificate as supervisor of an oil, chemical or
    liquefied gas transfer operation;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a Small Vessel Machinery Operator certificate;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} a Master, Limited certificate or Chief Mate,
    Limited certificate;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} a Ship's Cook certificate; or

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} a Restricted Proficiency in Survival Craft and
    Rescue Boats Other Than Fast Rescue Boats certificate.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The rights resulting from a certificate for use at
  sea and, as the case may be, from an endorsement to that certificate
  are subject to any limitations written on them and in the most recent
  medical certificate issued to the holder of the certificate.

### [Fees for Examinations and Documents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729672 .Subheading}

- **[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), an
  applicant shall pay the fee set out in column 2 of the table to this
  subsection for an examination set out in column 1.

  examination taken for the purpose of obtaining one of the following

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Small Vessel Machinery Operator certificate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a High-Speed Craft (HSC) Type Rating certificate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Type Rating
    certificate;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class I
    certificate; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class II
    certificate.

- **[[122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122

  an applicant shall pay the fee set out in column 2 of the table to
  this subsection for a document or an endorsement set out in column 1.

  issuance or replacement of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Small Vessel Machinery Operator certificate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a Ballast Control Operator certificate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a Passenger Safety Management certificate or
    endorsement;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a High-Speed Craft (HSC) Type Rating certificate;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Type Rating
    certificate;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class I
    certificate;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class II
    certificate;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any sailing vessel endorsement; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a passenger submersible craft endorsement.

  certificate.

### [Masters and Mates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729706 .Subheading}

#### [Master Mariner]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729707 .Subheading}

- **[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Master Mariner certificate shall meet the requirements
  set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and the
  corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729710">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Master, Near
  Coastal;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Master 3 000 Gross Tonnage,
  Near Coastal;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> Chief Mate;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> Watchkeeping Mate;
  or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> Watchkeeping Mate, Near
  Coastal.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> MED for senior
  officers;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> Restricted Operator’s
  Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
  href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> SEN Level II;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> electronic chart display and
  information system (ECDIS); and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> marine advanced first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>

  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Communications, level
  1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> communications, level
  2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> celestial navigation, level
  2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> navigation systems and
  instruments;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> deviascope;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> navigation safety, level
  2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> meteorology, level
  2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> ship management, level
  3;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> ship management, level
  4;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(j)</span> ship construction and
  stability, level 4;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(k)</span> ship construction and
  stability, level 5;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(l)</span> cargo, level 3;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(m)</span> engineering knowledge, level
  2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(n)</span> SIM II, after obtaining the
  certificate referred to in paragraph 3(f); and</p></li>

  seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
  </ul></td>

  </table>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} After obtaining a Master 500 Gross Tonnage, Near
  Coastal certificate, Watchkeeping Mate certificate or Watchkeeping
  Mate, Near Coastal certificate, an applicant for a Master Mariner
  certificate shall acquire the following sea service on board one or
  more vessels of at least 500 gross tonnage while the vessels are
  engaged on unlimited voyages or on near coastal voyages, Class 1,
  which sea service shall include at least 12 months on voyages outside
  the Great Lakes Basin and where the distance between extreme ports
  called at during those voyages is more than 500 nautical miles:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 36 months as officer in charge of the deck
    watch;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 24 months as officer in charge of the deck
    watch if they have served at least 12 months as master or chief
    mate; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an equivalent prorated combination of the
    requirements set out in paragraphs (a) and (b).

#### [Master, Near Coastal]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729720 .Subheading}

- **[[124]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Master, Near Coastal certificate shall meet the
  requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and
  the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729723">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Master 3 000 Gross Tonnage,
  Near Coastal;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Chief Mate;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> Chief Mate, Near
  Coastal;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> Watchkeeping Mate;
  or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> Watchkeeping Mate, Near
  Coastal.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">The sea service set out in
  subsection (2).</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> MED for senior
  officers;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> Restricted Operator’s
  Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
  href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> SEN Level II;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> electronic chart display and
  information system (ECDIS); and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> marine advanced first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Navigation systems and
  instruments;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> navigation safety,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> meteorology,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> ship management,
  level 3;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> ship management,
  level 4;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> ship construction and
  stability, level 4;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> ship construction and
  stability, level 5;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> cargo, level 3;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> engineering knowledge,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(j)</span> SIM II, after obtaining the
  certificate referred to in paragraph 3(f); and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(k)</span> oral examination on general
  seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} After obtaining a Master 500 Gross Tonnage, Near
  Coastal certificate, Watchkeeping Mate certificate or Watchkeeping
  Mate, Near Coastal certificate, an applicant for a Master, Near
  Coastal certificate shall acquire the following sea service on board
  one or more vessels of at least 500 gross tonnage while the vessels
  are engaged on voyages other than sheltered waters voyages:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 36 months as officer in charge of the deck
    watch;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 24 months as officer in charge of the deck
    watch if they have served at least 12 months as master or chief
    mate; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an equivalent prorated combination of the
    requirements set out in paragraphs (a) and (b).

  age.

  endorsement to a Master 500 Gross Tonnage, Domestic certificate shall
  acquire at least 6 months of sea service on board one or more vessels
  of at least 25 gross tonnage while the vessels are engaged on voyages
  other than sheltered waters voyages, which service, in part or in
  whole, may have been counted toward meeting the requirement of item 2
  of the table to subsection (1).

#### [Master 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729774 .Subheading}

- **[[129]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-129 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Master 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic certificate shall
  meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this
  subsection and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729777">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
