  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Acquire the following sea service on board
  one or more vessels of at least 5 gross tonnage:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if the applicant has
  successfully completed an approved training program,</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> at least 12 months performing
  deck duties, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> at least 6 months performing
  deck duties while holding a Chief Mate 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic
  certificate;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if Part 2 requires the
  vessels on which the service was acquired to have on board a chief mate,
  at least 6 months as chief mate while holding a Chief Mate 150 Gross
  Tonnage, Domestic certificate; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> in all other cases</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> at least 24 months performing
  deck duties,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> at least 12 months
  performing deck duties while holding a Chief Mate 150 Gross Tonnage,
  Domestic certificate, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> an equivalent prorated
  combination of the requirements set out in paragraph (b) and
  subparagraph (c)(ii).</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> Restricted Operator’s
  Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
  href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> SEN limited; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> marine advanced first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Chartwork and pilotage,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> navigation safety,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> meteorology,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> ship construction and
  stability, level 3;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> general ship knowledge,
  level 3; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> oral examination on general
  seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a Limited, Contiguous Waters Voyage
  endorsement to a Master 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic certificate shall
  acquire the following amount of sea service on board one or more
  vessels of at least 5 gross tonnage while the vessels are engaged on
  voyages other than sheltered waters voyages, which service, in part or
  in whole, may have been counted toward meeting the requirement of item
  2 of the table to subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 12 months; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 6 months if the applicant has successfully
    completed an approved training program.

#### [Master, Limited for a Vessel of 60 Gross Tonnage or More]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729786 .Subheading}

**[[130]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-130 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Master, Limited certificate for a vessel of 60 gross tonnage or more
shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this
section and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="729788">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Master 150 Gross Tonnage,
Domestic;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Watchkeeping Mate;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> Watchkeeping Mate, Near
Coastal;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> Chief Mate 500 Gross Tonnage,
Domestic;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> Chief Mate 150 Gross Tonnage,
Domestic; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> Chief Mate, Limited
certificate for a vessel of 60 gross tonnage or more, if the vessel is
required under Part 2 to have on board a chief mate.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Acquire the following sea service on board
one or more vessels engaged on voyages that correspond to those that the
vessel in respect of which the certificate is sought is permitted to
engage on:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> in the case where the
certificate is to be valid on board a vessel of 150 gross tonnage or
more,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> if Part 2 does not require
the vessels to have on board a chief mate and the vessels are of at
least 25 gross tonnage,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(A)</span> at least 12 months performing
deck duties, or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(B)</span> at least 6 months from an
approved program of on-board training, or</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> if Part 2 requires the
vessels to have on board a chief mate and the vessels are of at least 60
gross tonnage, as chief mate while holding one of the certificates
listed in item 1,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(A)</span> at least 6 months,
or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(B)</span> at least 3 months from an
approved program of on-board training; or</p></li>
</ul></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in the case where the
certificate is to be valid on board a vessel of less than 150 gross
tonnage,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> if the vessels are not
required under Part 2 to have on board a chief mate, on one or more
vessels of at least 25 gross tonnage,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(A)</span> at least 6 months performing
deck duties, or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(B)</span> at least 3 months from an
approved program of on-board training, or</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> if the vessels are required
under Part 2 to have on board a chief mate, on one or more vessels of at
least 60 gross tonnage, as chief mate while holding one of the
certificates listed in item 1,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(A)</span> at least 3 months,
or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(B)</span> at least 6 weeks from an
approved program of on-board training.</p></li>
</ul></li>
</ul></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
the examiner or, if specified, examinations to be passed in lieu of
those certificates</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> If the vessel is a
passenger-carrying vessel that does not carry a fireman’s outfit, MED
with respect to small passenger vessel safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if the vessel is not a
passenger-carrying vessel and does not carry a fireman’s outfit,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> MED with respect to basic
safety, or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> a practical examination on
MED using the vessel’s emergency equipment;</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> if the vessel has boat or
life-raft launching equipment, MED training with respect to survival
craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue boats;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> if the vessel is carrying a
fireman’s outfit, MED with respect to STCW basic safety instead of the
training or examination required by either paragraph (a) or
(b);</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> if the vessel is a ferry that
has more than one enclosed deck and that either is used outside of the
period commencing on March 31 and ending on December 1 or engages on
voyages other than sheltered waters voyage, the following courses:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> MED in advanced fire
fighting, and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> MED for senior
officers;</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> SEN Limited if the vessel is
fitted with a radar;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> marine basic first aid;
and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> an appropriate radio operator
certificate issued under the <a href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication
Act</a> if the vessel is equipped with a VHF radiotelephone
installation.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
<td>Examinations on subject-matter appropriate to the area of operation
and the type and gross tonnage of the vessel to which the certificate
relates as set out in TP 2293.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Master, Limited for a Vessel of Less Than 60 Gross Tonnage]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729794 .Subheading}

**[[131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Master, Limited certificate for a vessel of less than 60 gross tonnage
shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this
section and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="729796">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Acquire, while performing deck duties on
board one or more vessels of a gross tonnage at least equivalent to that
of the vessel in respect of which the certificate is sought while the
vessels are engaged on voyages that correspond to those that the vessel
in respect of which the certificate is sought is permitted to engage
on,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 2 months of sea
service; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> at least 1 month of sea
service from an approved program of on-board training.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
the examiner or, if specified, examination to be passed in lieu of those
certificates</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> If the vessel is a
passenger-carrying vessel,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> MED with respect to small
passenger vessel safety,</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> MED with respect to basic
safety and MED with respect to small seasonal passenger vessel safety
(certificated personnel), or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> MED with respect to small
seasonal passenger vessel safety (certificated personnel) and MED with
respect to small seasonal passenger vessel safety (non-certificated
personnel);</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if the vessel is not a
passenger-carrying vessel,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> MED with respect to basic
safety, or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> a practical examination on
MED using the vessel’s emergency equipment;</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> an appropriate radio operator
certificate issued under the <a href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication
Act</a> if the vessel is equipped with a VHF radiotelephone
installation; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> marine basic first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
<td>Examinations on subject-matter appropriate to the area of operation
and the type and gross tonnage of the vessel to which the certificate
relates as set out in TP 2293.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Chief Mate]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729802 .Subheading}

- **[[132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Chief Mate certificate shall meet the requirements set
  out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and the corresponding
  specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729805">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Chief Mate, Near
  Coastal;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Watchkeeping Mate;
  or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> Watchkeeping Mate, Near
  Coastal.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">The sea service set out in
  subsection (2).</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> MED for senior
  officers;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> Restricted Operator’s
  Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
  href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> SEN Level II; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> marine advanced first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Celestial navigation,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> navigation systems and
  instruments;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> deviascope;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> navigation safety,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> meteorology,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> ship management,
  level 3;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> ship construction and
  stability, level 4;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> cargo, level 3;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> engineering knowledge,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(j)</span> SIM II, after obtaining the
  certificate referred to in paragraph 3(f); and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(k)</span> oral examination on general
  seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} After obtaining a Master 500 Gross Tonnage, Near
  Coastal certificate, Watchkeeping Mate certificate or Watchkeeping
  Mate, Near Coastal certificate, an applicant for a Chief Mate
  certificate shall acquire at least 12 months of sea service as officer
  in charge of the deck watch, on board one or more vessels of at least
  500 gross tonnage, while the vessels are engaged on voyages that are
  unlimited voyages or near coastal voyages, Class 1, which sea service
  shall include at least 6 months on voyages outside the Great Lakes
  Basin and where the distance between extreme ports called at during
  those voyages is more than 500 nautical miles.

#### [Chief Mate, Near Coastal]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729812 .Subheading}

**[[133]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-133 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Chief Mate, Near Coastal certificate shall meet the requirements set out
in column 1 of the table to this section and the corresponding
specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="729814">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Watchkeeping Mate;
or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Watchkeeping Mate, Near
Coastal.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">After obtaining a Master 500 Gross Tonnage,
Near Coastal certificate, Watchkeeping Mate certificate or Watchkeeping
Mate, Near Coastal certificate, acquire at least 12 months of sea
service as officer in charge of the deck watch, on board one or more
vessels of at least 500 gross tonnage, while the vessels are engaged on
voyages other than sheltered waters voyages.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
the examiner</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
basic safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
boats;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
fighting;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> MED for senior
officers;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> Restricted Operator’s
Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> SEN Level II; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> marine advanced first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
<td><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Navigation systems and
instruments;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> navigation safety,
level 2;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> meteorology,
level 2;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> ship management,
level 3;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> ship construction and
stability, level 4;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> cargo, level 3;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> engineering knowledge,
level 1;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> SIM II, after obtaining the
certificate referred to in paragraph 3(f); and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> oral examination on general
seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Watchkeeping Mate and Watchkeeping Mate, Near Coastal]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729820 .Subheading}

- **[[134]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Watchkeeping Mate or Watchkeeping Mate, Near Coastal
  certificate shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the
  table to this subsection and the corresponding specifications set out
  in column 2.

  <figure id="729823">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">The sea service set out in
  subsection (2).</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates and other
  documents to be provided to the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> Restricted Operator’s
  Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
  href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> SEN Level I;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> electronic chart display and
  information system (ECDIS);</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> marine advanced first
  aid;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> if applying for a
  Watchkeeping Mate certificate, knowledge and use of a marine sextant;
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> a steering testimonial
  attesting to the applicant’s ability to steer and containing the
  declaration and at least the information set out in Schedule 3 to this
  Part.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Communications,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> communications,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> chartwork and pilotage,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> navigation safety,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> meteorology,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> ship construction and
  stability, level 4;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> cargo, level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> general ship knowledge,
  level 3;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> SIM I, after obtaining the
  certificate referred to in paragraph 2(e);</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(j)</span> celestial navigation, level
  2, in the case of an applicant for a Watchkeeping Mate certificate,
  after obtaining the certificate required under paragraph 2(h);
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(k)</span> oral examination on general
  seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a Watchkeeping Mate or Watchkeeping
  Mate, Near Coastal certificate shall acquire the following sea service
  performing deck duties on board one or more vessels of at least 25
  gross tonnage while the vessels are engaged on voyages other than
  sheltered waters voyages:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 36 months, including at least 6 months
    performing bridge watchkeeping duties under the supervision of a
    qualified deck officer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 24 months from an approved program of
    on-board training; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at least 12 months from an approved cadet training
    program in navigation.

#### [Chief Mate 500 Gross Tonnage, Domestic]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729833 .Subheading}

- **[[135]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Chief Mate 500 Gross Tonnage, Domestic certificate
  shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this
  subsection and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729836">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Acquire the following sea service on board
  one or more vessels of at least 5 gross tonnage:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> if the applicant has
  successfully completed an approved training program,</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> at least 12 months performing
  deck duties, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> at least 6 months performing
  deck duties while holding a Chief Mate 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic
  certificate;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if Part 2 requires the
  vessels on which the service was acquired to have on board a chief mate,
  at least 6 months as chief mate while holding a Chief Mate 150 Gross
  Tonnage, Domestic certificate; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> in all other cases,</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> at least 24 months performing
  deck duties,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> at least 12 months
  performing deck duties while holding a Chief Mate 150 Gross Tonnage,
  Domestic certificate, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> an equivalent prorated
  combination of the requirements set out in paragraph (b) and
  subparagraph (c)(ii).</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> Restricted Operator’s
  Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
  href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> SEN Level I; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> marine advanced first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Communications,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> chartwork and pilotage,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> navigation safety,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> ship construction and
  stability, level 3;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> general ship knowledge,
  level 3;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> SIM I, after obtaining the
  certificate referred to in paragraph 2(e); and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> oral examination on general
  seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a Limited, Contiguous Waters Voyage
  endorsement to a Chief Mate 500 Gross Tonnage, Domestic certificate
  shall acquire the following amount of sea service on one or more
  vessels of at least 5 gross tonnage while the vessels are engaged on
  voyages other than sheltered waters voyages, which service, in part or
  in whole, may have been counted toward meeting the requirement of item
  1 of the table to subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 12 months; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 6 months if the applicant has successfully
    completed an approved training program.

#### [Chief Mate 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729845 .Subheading}

- **[[136]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Chief Mate 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic certificate
  shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this
  subsection and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729848">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Acquire the following amount of sea
  service performing deck duties on one or more vessels of at least 5
  gross tonnage:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 12 months;
  or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> at least 6 months if the
  applicant has successfully completed an approved training
  program.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> Restricted Operator’s
  Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
  href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> SEN limited; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> marine advanced first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Chartwork and pilotage,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> navigation safety, level 1;
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> oral examination on general
  seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a Limited, Contiguous Waters Voyage
  endorsement to a Chief Mate 150 Gross Tonnage, Domestic certificate
  shall acquire the following amount of sea service on board one or more
  vessels of at least 5 gross tonnage while the vessels are engaged on
  voyages other than sheltered waters voyages, which service, in part or
  in whole, may have been counted toward meeting the requirement of item
  2 of the table to subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 6 months; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 3 months if the applicant has successfully
    completed an approved training program.

#### [Chief Mate, Limited for a Vessel of 60 Gross Tonnage or More]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729857 .Subheading}

**[[137]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Chief Mate, Limited certificate for a vessel of 60 gross tonnage or more
shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this
section and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="729859">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(1)</span> In the case where the
certificate is to be valid on board a vessel of 150 gross tonnage or
more, subject to subparagraph (b)(i), the following sea service on one
or more vessels of at least 25 gross tonnage while the vessels are
engaged on voyages that correspond to those that the vessel in respect
of which the certificate is sought is permitted to engage on:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 6 months performing
deck duties;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> a total of at least 6 months,
consisting of</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> up to 3 months as master or
chief mate while holding a certificate that authorizes the applicant to
be in charge of the deck watch, which may be served on one or more
vessels of at least 5 gross tonnage, and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> at least 3 months performing
deck duties; or</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> at least 3 months from an
approved program of on-board training.</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(2)</span> In the case where the
certificate is to be valid on board a vessel of less than 150 gross
tonnage, subject to subparagraph (b)(i), the following sea service on
one or more vessels of at least 25 gross tonnage while the vessels are
engaged on voyages that correspond to those that the vessel in respect
of which the certificate is sought is permitted to engage on:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 3 months performing
deck duties;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> a total of at least 3 months,
consisting of</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> up to 6 weeks as master or
chief mate while holding a certificate that authorizes the applicant to
be in charge of the deck watch, which may be served on one or more
vessels of at least 5 gross tonnage, and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> at least 6 weeks performing
deck duties; or</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> at least 6 weeks from an
approved program of on-board training.</p></li>
</ul></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
the examiner or, if specified, examinations to be passed in lieu of
those certificates</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> If the vessel is a
passenger-carrying vessel that does not carry a fireman’s outfit, MED
with respect to small passenger vessel safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if the vessel is not a
passenger-carrying vessel and does not carry a fireman’s outfit,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> MED with respect to basic
safety, or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> a practical examination on
MED using the vessel’s emergency equipment;</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> if the vessel has boat or
life-raft launching equipment, MED training with respect to survival
craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue boats;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> if the vessel is carrying a
fireman’s outfit, MED with respect to STCW basic safety instead of the
training or examination required by either paragraph (a) or
(b);</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> if the vessel is a ferry that
has more than one enclosed deck and that either is used outside of the
period commencing on March 31 and ending on December 1 or engages on
voyages other than sheltered waters voyages, the following courses:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> MED in advanced fire
fighting, and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> MED for senior
officers;</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> SEN limited if the vessel is
fitted with a radar;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> marine basic first aid;
and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> an appropriate radio operator
certificate issued under the <a href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication
Act</a> if the vessel is equipped with a VHF radiotelephone
installation.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
<td>Examinations on subject-matter appropriate to the area of operation
and the type and gross tonnage of the vessel to which the certificate
relates as set out in TP 2293.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Chief Mate, Limited for a Vessel of Less Than 60 Gross Tonnage]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729865 .Subheading}

**[[138]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Chief Mate, Limited certificate for a vessel of less than 60 gross
tonnage shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to
this section and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="729867">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Acquire at least 1 month of sea service, or
at least two weeks of sea service from an approved program of on-board
training, while performing deck duties on board one or more vessels of a
gross tonnage at least equivalent to that of the vessel in respect of
which the certificate is sought while the vessels are engaged on voyages
that correspond to those that the vessel in respect of which the
certificate is sought is permitted to engage on.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
the examiner or, if specified, examination to be passed in lieu of those
certificates</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> If the vessel is a
passenger-carrying vessel,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> MED with respect to small
passenger vessel safety,</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> MED with respect to basic
safety and MED with respect to small seasonal passenger vessel safety
(certificated personnel), or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> MED with respect to small
seasonal passenger vessel safety (certificated personnel) and MED with
respect to small seasonal passenger vessel safety (non-certificated
personnel);</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if the vessel is not a
passenger-carrying vessel,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> MED with respect to basic
safety, or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> a practical examination on
MED using the vessel’s emergency equipment;</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> an appropriate radio operator
certificate issued under the <a href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication
Act</a> if the vessel is equipped with a VHF radiotelephone
installation; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> marine basic first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
<td>Examinations on subject-matter appropriate to the area of operation
and the type and gross tonnage of the vessel to which the certificate
relates as set out in TP 2293.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Fishing Master, First Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729873 .Subheading}

**[[139]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-139 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Fishing Master, First Class certificate shall meet the requirements set
out in column 1 of the table to this section and the corresponding
specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="729875">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Watchkeeping Mate;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Watchkeeping Mate, Near
Coastal; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> Fishing Master, Second
Class.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>After obtaining a certificate referred to
in item 1, acquire at least 12 months of sea service on board one or
more fishing vessels of at least 25 gross tonnage while the vessels are
engaged on unlimited voyages or near coastal voyages, Class 1, up to 6
months of which may be substituted by service as officer in charge of
the deck watch on one or more normal trading vessels of at least 25
gross tonnage, as</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> officer in charge of the deck
watch; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> master.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
the examiner</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
basic safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
boats;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
fighting;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> MED for senior
officers;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> Restricted Operator’s
Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> SEN Level II; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> marine advanced first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
<td><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Communications,
level 2;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> celestial navigation,
level 1;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> navigation safety,
level 2;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> meteorology,
level 2;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> ship management,
level 1;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> general ship knowledge,
level 2;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> SIM II, after obtaining the
certificate referred to in paragraph 3(f); and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> oral examination on general
seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Fishing Master, Second Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729881 .Subheading}

**[[140]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Fishing Master, Second Class certificate shall meet the requirements set
out in column 1 of the table to this section and the corresponding
specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="729883">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Watchkeeping Mate;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Watchkeeping Mate, Near
Coastal; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> Fishing Master, Third
Class.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>While holding a certificate referred to in
item 1, acquire at least 12 months of sea service on board one or more
fishing vessels of at least 25 gross tonnage while the vessels are
engaged on voyages other than sheltered waters voyages, up to 6 months
of which may be substituted by service as officer in charge of the deck
watch on one or more normal trading vessels of at least 25 gross
tonnage, as</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> officer in charge of the deck
watch; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> master.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
the examiner</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
basic safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
boats;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
fighting;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> MED for senior
officers;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> Restricted Operator’s
Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> SEN Level I; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> marine advanced first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
<td><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Ship management,
level 1;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> general ship knowledge,
level 1;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> SIM I, after obtaining the
certificate referred to in paragraph 3(f); and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> oral examination on general
seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Fishing Master, Third Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729889 .Subheading}

- **[[141]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-141 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Fishing Master, Third Class certificate shall meet the
  requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and
  the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729892">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Acquire the following sea service
  performing deck duties on one or more fishing vessels of at least 25
  gross tonnage while the vessels are engaged on voyages other than
  sheltered waters voyages:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 24 months, which may
  include up to 12 months from an approved training program, if the
  applicant has successfully completed that program; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> at least 12 months while
  holding a Fishing Master, Fourth Class certificate.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to basic
  safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Restricted Operator’s
  Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
  href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> SEN limited; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> marine advanced first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Communications, level
  1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> chartwork and pilotage,
  level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> navigation safety,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> meteorology,
  level 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> ship construction and
  stability, level 2;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> general ship knowledge, level
  1; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> oral examination on general
  seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite item 1 of the table to subsection (1), up to
  half the amount of an applicant's sea service performing deck duties
  required by that item, excluding any service from an approved training
  program, may be substituted by service on board one or more normal
  trading vessels of at least 25 gross tonnage.

#### [Fishing Master, Fourth Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729899 .Subheading}

- **[[142]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Fishing Master, Fourth Class certificate shall meet
  the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection
  and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729902">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">Acquire at least 12 months of sea service
  performing deck duties on one or more fishing vessels that are 6 m or
  more in length, which service may include up to 6 months in an approved
  training program if the applicant has successfully completed that
  program.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to basic
  safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Restricted Operator’s
  Certificate (ROC-MC) issued under the <a
  href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication Act</a>;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> SEN limited; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> marine advanced first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Chartwork and pilotage, level
  1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> navigation safety, level
  1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> ship construction and
  stability, level 1; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> oral examination on general
  seamanship, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite item 1 of the table to subsection (1), up to
  half the amount of an applicant's sea service performing deck duties
  required by that item, excluding any service from an approved training
  program, may include service on board one or more normal trading
  vessels of 6 m or more in length.

#### [Certificate of Service as Master of a Fishing Vessel of Less Than 60 Gross Tonnage]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729909 .Subheading}

- **[[143]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Certificate of Service as Master of a Fishing Vessel
  of Less Than 60 Gross Tonnage shall meet the requirements set out in
  column 1 of the table to this subsection and the corresponding
  specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729912">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">At least 12 months of sea service acquired
  before the coming into force of this section as master of one or more
  fishing vessels of at least 15 gross tonnage or at least 12 m in overall
  length while engaged on voyages that the certificate sought would
  permit.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Certificates to be provided to the
  examiner</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to basic
  safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if the vessel is equipped
  with a VHF radiotelephone installation, an appropriate radio operator
  certificate issued under the <a href="/eng/acts/R-2">Radiocommunication
  Act</a>; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> if the applicant has not
  acquired at least seven fishing seasons of experience as master of one
  or more fishing vessels, with no two of those seasons having occurred in
  the same year,</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> SEN limited,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> marine basic first aid,
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> small vessel operator
  proficiency.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) expires 10 years after the day on
  which this section comes into force.

### [Engineering Officers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-729919 .Subheading}

#### [First-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729920 .Subheading}

- **[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a First-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship
  certificate who holds a Second-class Engineer certificate for the same
  type of vessel to which the certificate sought relates shall meet the
  requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and
  the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729923">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top"></th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">Second-class Engineer certificate for the
  same type of vessel to which the certificate sought relates.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>While holding a Second-class Engineer,
  Motor Ship or Steamship certificate, in accordance with the certificate
  sought, acquire at least 15 months of qualifying service as follows:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 9 months of sea
  service as engineer in charge of the engineering watch or in charge of
  the machinery</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> on one or more motor vessels
  that have a propulsive power of at least 3 000 kW, other than a
  stationary MOU, in the case of an applicant for a First-class Engineer,
  Motor Ship certificate, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> on one or more steamships
  that have a propulsive power of at least 3 000 kW, in the case of an
  applicant for a First-class Engineer, Steamship certificate;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> any remaining time in any
  combination of the following types of service on one or more of the
  following vessels that have a power of at least 1 500 kW:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> service as an engineer on
  board a motor vessel or steamship,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> service as an engineer on
  board an MOU without propulsion systems, credited at a rate of one-half
  the time served counting as qualifying service, and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> up to 3 months of
  qualifying service in respect of time spent in laying up, fitting out or
  refitting on board a vessel at a maximum rate of one day of service for
  each calendar day of at least 8 hours of work; and</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> up to 3 months of qualifying
  service in respect of attendance at a program or course, other than an
  approved training course, at a maximum rate of one day of service for
  every three days of attendance, if</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> the course or program
  substantially covers the subject-matter of at least one of the
  examinations referred to in item 4, and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> the applicant provides a
  testimonial indicating that the applicant has successfully completed
  that program or course.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> MED for senior
  officers;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> marine advanced first aid;
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> ship management practices
  taught using a propulsive plant simulator.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> The following written
  examinations at the first-class engineer level, while holding a
  Second-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship certificate:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> applied mechanics,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> thermodynamics,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> electrotechnology,
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iv)</span> naval architecture;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> examination on ship
  management practices using a propulsive plant simulator, after providing
  the certificate referred to in paragraph 3(f);</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> written examination on
  general engineering knowledge at the first-class level, after meeting
  the requirements of items 2 and 3 and paragraphs (a) and (b);</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> one of the following written
  examinations at the first-class level, after meeting the requirements of
  paragraph (c):</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> engineering knowledge of
  motor vessels, in the case of an applicant for a First-class Engineer,
  Motor Ship certificate, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> engineering knowledge of
  steamships, in the case of an applicant for a First-class Engineer,
  Steamship certificate; and</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> oral examination on the
  knowledge set out in paragraphs (a) to (d) and a knowledge of the
  legislation relevant to the certificate sought, after passing the
  examination referred to in paragraph (d).</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a First-class Engineer, Motor Ship
  certificate who holds a First-class Engineer, Steamship certificate
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} after obtaining a Second-class Engineer, Motor Ship
    or Steamship certificate, acquire at least 6 months of service as an
    engineer on one or more motor vessels that have a propulsive power
    of at least 3 000 kW other than stationary MOUs; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pass a written and an oral examination to determine
    whether they have the engineering knowledge required by subparagraph
    4(d)(i) of the table to subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a First-class Engineer, Steamship
  certificate who holds a First-class Engineer, Motor Ship certificate
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} after obtaining a Second-class Engineer, Motor Ship
    or Steamship certificate, acquire at least 6 months of service as an
    engineer on one or more steamships that have a propulsive power of
    at least 3 000 kW; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pass a written and an oral examination to determine
    whether they have the engineering knowledge required by subparagraph
    4(d)(ii) of the table to subsection (1).

#### [Second-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729935 .Subheading}

- **[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Second-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship
  certificate who holds a Fourth-class Engineer certificate with STCW
  endorsement or a Third-class Engineer certificate for the same type of
  vessel to which the certificate sought relates shall meet the
  requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and
  the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="729938">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">Fourth-class Engineer certificate with STCW
  endorsement.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>After acquiring the qualifying service
  required for a Third-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship
  certificate, acquire at least 12 months of additional qualifying service
  as follows:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 6 months of sea
  service as engineer in charge of the engineering watch or in charge of
  machinery on one or more of the following vessels that have a propulsive
  power of at least 750 kW:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> on board a motor vessel other
  than a stationary MOU, in the case of an applicant for a motor ship
  certificate, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> on board a steamship, in the
  case of an applicant for a steamship certificate;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> up to 3 months of qualifying
  service in respect of attendance at a program or course, other than an
  approved training course, at a maximum rate of one day of service for
  every three days of attendance, if</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> the program or course
  substantially covers the subject-matter of at least one of the
  examinations referred to in item 4, and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> the applicant provides a
  testimonial indicating that the applicant has successfully completed
  that program or course; and</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> any remaining time in any
  combination of the following types of service on one or more of the
  following vessels that have a power of at least 750 kW:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> service as an engineer on
  board a steamship or motor vessel,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> service as an engineer on
  board an MOU that does not have a propulsion system accrued at a rate of
  one-half the time served counting as qualifying service, and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> up to 3 months of
  qualifying service in respect of time spent in laying up, fitting out or
  refitting a vessel at a rate of a maximum of one day of qualifying
  service for each calendar day of at least 8 hours of work.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> MED for senior
  officers;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> marine advanced first aid;
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> ship management practices
  taught using a propulsive plant simulator.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> The following written
  examinations at the second-class engineer level, while holding a
  Fourth-class Engineer certificate:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> applied mechanics,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> thermodynamics,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> electrotechnology,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iv)</span> naval architecture,
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(v)</span> technical drawing;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> after providing the
  certificate referred to in paragraph 3(f), an examination on ship
  management practices using a propulsive plant simulator;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> written examination on
  general engineering knowledge at the second-class level, after meeting
  the requirements of items 2 and 3 and paragraphs (a) and (b);</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> one of the following written
  examinations at the second-class level, after meeting the requirements
  of paragraph (c):</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> engineering knowledge of
  motor vessels, in the case of an applicant for a Second-class Engineer,
  Motor Ship certificate, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> engineering knowledge of
  steamships, in the case of an applicant for a Second-class Engineer,
  Steamship certificate; and</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> oral examination on the
  knowledge set out in paragraphs (a) to (d) and a knowledge of the
  legislation relevant to the certificate sought, after passing the
  examination referred to in paragraph (d).</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a Second-class Engineer, Motor Ship
  certificate who holds a First-class or Second-class Engineer,
  Steamship certificate shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} after obtaining a Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship
    or Steamship certificate, acquire at least 6 months of service as an
    engineer on one or more motor vessels that have a propulsive power
    of at least 750 kW other than stationary MOUs; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pass a written and an oral examination to determine
    whether they have the engineering knowledge required by subparagraph
    4(d)(i) of the table to subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a Second-class Engineer, Steamship
  certificate who holds a First-class or Second-class Engineer, Motor
  Ship certificate shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} after obtaining a Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship
    or Steamship certificate, acquire at least 6 months of service as an
    engineer on one or more steamships that have a propulsive power of
    at least 750 kW; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pass a written and an oral examination to determine
    whether they have the engineering knowledge required by subparagraph
    4(d)(ii) of the table to subsection (1).

#### [Third-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729950 .Subheading}

- **[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Third-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship
  certificate who holds a Fourth-class Engineer certificate with STCW
  endorsement for the same type of vessel to which the certificate
  sought relates shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the
  table to this subsection and the corresponding specifications set out
  in column 2.

  <figure id="729953">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">Fourth-class Engineer certificate with STCW
  endorsement.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>After having acquired the qualifying
  service required for a Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship
  certificate, acquire at least 12 months of qualifying service as
  follows:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 6 months of sea
  service as engineer in charge of the engineering watch or in charge of
  the machinery on one or more of the following vessels that have a
  propulsive power of at least 500 kW:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> a motor vessel other than a
  stationary MOU, in the case of an applicant for a motor ship
  certificate, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> on a steamship, in the case
  of an applicant for a steamship certificate;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> up to 3 months of qualifying
  service in respect of attendance at a program or course, other than an
  approved training course, at a maximum rate of one day of service for
  every three days of attendance, if</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> the program or course
  substantially covers the subject-matter of at least one of the
  examinations referred to in item 4, and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> the applicant provides a
  testimonial indicating that the applicant has successfully completed
  that program or course; and</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> any remaining time in a
  combination of the following types of service on one or more of the
  following vessels that have a power of at least 500 kW:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> up to 3 months as an engineer
  on day work on board a vessel,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> up to 3 months of qualifying
  service in respect of time spent in laying up, fitting out or refitting
  as an engineer on board a vessel to be credited at a maximum rate of one
  day of qualifying service for each calendar day of at least 8 hours of
  work,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> service as an engineer on
  board a vessel without a propulsion system that is a motor or steam
  dredge, an MOU, a floating elevator or a similar vessel, to be credited
  at a rate of one-half the time served counting as qualifying service,
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iv)</span> service as an engine-room
  rating performing watchkeeping duties in an engine room on board a motor
  vessel or steamship, to be credited at a rate of one-third the time
  served counting as qualifying service.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
