  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> MED for senior
  officers;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> marine advanced first aid;
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> ship watchkeeping practices
  taught using a propulsive plant simulator.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> The following written
  examinations at the third-class engineer level, after obtaining a
  Fourth-class Engineer certificate:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> applied mechanics,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> thermodynamics,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> electrotechnology,
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iv)</span> applied
  mathematics;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> an examination on ship
  watchkeeping practices using a propulsive plant simulator, after
  providing the certificate referred to in paragraph 3(f);</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> written examination on
  general engineering knowledge at the third-class level, after meeting
  the requirements of items 2 and 3 and paragraphs (a) and (b);</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> one of the following written
  examinations at the third-class level, after meeting the requirements of
  paragraph (c):</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> engineering knowledge of
  motor vessels, in the case of an applicant for a Third-class Engineer,
  Motor Ship certificate, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> engineering knowledge of
  steamships, in the case of an applicant for a Third-class Engineer,
  Steamship certificate; and</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> oral examination on the
  knowledge set out in paragraphs (a) to (d) and a knowledge of the
  legislation relevant to the certificate sought, after passing the
  examination referred to in paragraph (d).</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a Third-class Engineer, Motor Ship
  certificate who holds a First-class, Second-class or Third-class
  Engineer, Steamship certificate shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} after obtaining a Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship
    or Steamship certificate, acquire at least 6 months of service as an
    engineer on one or more motor vessels that have a propulsive power
    of at least 500 kW other than stationary MOUs; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pass a written and an oral examination to determine
    whether they have the engineering knowledge required by subparagraph
    4(d)(i) of the table to subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a Third-class Engineer, Steamship
  certificate who holds a First-class, Second-class or Third-class
  Engineer, Motor Ship certificate shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} after obtaining a Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship
    or Steamship certificate, acquire at least 6 months of service as an
    engineer on one or more steamships that have a propulsive power of
    at least 500 kW; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pass a written and an oral examination to determine
    whether they have the engineering knowledge required by subparagraph
    4(d)(ii) of the table to subsection (1).

#### [Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729965 .Subheading}

- **[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship
  certificate shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the
  table to this subsection and the corresponding specifications set out
  in column 2.

  <figure id="729968">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">No certificate is required, but the
  requirements set out in subsections (3) and (4) apply to the
  establishment of equivalency between the certificate held and the
  certificate sought.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Either having successfully completed a
  3-year approved cadet training program in marine engineering or having
  acquired at least 36 months of qualifying service comprising the
  following:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 6 months as an
  engineer, engine-room rating or assistant engineer, performing the
  duties set out in subsection (2) in an engine room on one or more of the
  following vessels the main engines of which have a total power of at
  least 500 kW:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> a motor vessel, in the case
  of an applicant for a motor ship certificate, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> a steamship, in the case of
  an applicant for a steamship certificate;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> a credit of 6 months of
  qualifying service if the applicant submits the certificate referred to
  in subparagraph 3(f)(i); and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> any remaining time in any
  combination of the following types of service:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> up to 12 months of fitting,
  erecting or repairing machinery,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> up to 6 months of metal
  turning,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> up to 6 months of brass
  finishing,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iv)</span> up to 6 months of planing,
  slotting, shaping and milling,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(v)</span> up to 3 months of
  welding,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(vi)</span> up to 6 months in a drafting
  office as mechanical or electrical drafter engaged in arrangement,
  detail or design drawings,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(vii)</span> up to 24 months as an
  engineer or assistant engineer on day work,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(viii)</span> up to 6 months as an
  engineer, engine-room rating, assistant engineer or electrician during
  the fitting out, laying up or refitting of one or more vessels,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ix)</span> up to 24 months as the
  person responsible for operating pumps on one or more tankers,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(x)</span> up to 24 months as an
  engine-room rating or assistant engineer performing watchkeeping duties
  in an engine room on board one or more towed barges or similar vessels,
  the boiler or boilers of which have a total heating surface of at least
  92.9 m<sup>2</sup>,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(xi)</span> up to 9 months as person
  responsible for operating tunnel machinery on one or more self-unloading
  bulk cargo ships,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(xii)</span> up to 24 months as an
  electrician on one or more vessels that have a rated generator capacity
  of at least 300 kW,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(xiii)</span> a credit of 12 months of
  service for successful completion of an approved training program in
  diesel engines,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(xiv)</span> a credit of up to 3 months
  of service for each of the following courses that has been successfully
  completed, at an institution recognized by a provincial government or
  foreign administration:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(A)</span> applied mechanics,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(B)</span> thermodynamics,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(C)</span> machine design,</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(D)</span> electrotechnology,
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(E)</span> naval architecture,
  and</p></li>
  </ul>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(xv)</span> a credit of 12 months of
  service for successful completion, at an institution recognized by a
  provincial government or foreign administration, of a training program
  in mechanical or electrical engineering.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates and other
  documents to be provided to the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
  proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
  boats;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED in advanced fire
  fighting;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> marine advanced first
  aid;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> ship watchkeeping practices
  taught using a propulsive plant simulator; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> for the sole purpose of
  obtaining a certificate with an STCW endorsement,</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> practical skills for marine
  engineers, and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> approved training record
  book for applicants for a Fourth-class Engineer certificate completed
  under the supervision of the vessel’s chief engineer.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> An examination on ship
  watchkeeping practices using a propulsive plant simulator, after
  providing the certificate referred to in paragraph 3(e);</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> after meeting the
  requirements of items 2 and 3 and paragraph (a), a written examination
  on general engineering knowledge at the fourth-class level;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> one of the following written
  examinations at the fourth-class level, after meeting the requirements
  of paragraph (b):</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> in the case of an applicant
  for a Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship certificate, engineering
  knowledge of motor vessels, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> in the case of an applicant
  for a Fourth-class Engineer, Steamship certificate, engineering
  knowledge of steamships; and</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> oral examination on the
  knowledge set out in paragraphs (a) to (c) and knowledge of the
  legislation relevant to the certificate sought, after passing the
  examination referred to in paragraph (c).</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The qualifying service set out in paragraph 2(a) of
  the table to subsection (1) shall include the following duties:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} preparing main machinery and auxiliary equipment
    for sea;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shutting down main machinery;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} operating main machinery;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} preparing, starting, coupling and changing over
    alternators and generators;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} transferring fuel;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} preparing and operating evaporators and
    distillation plants;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} operating oily water separators and conducting
    appropriate tests to ensure the correct operation of those
    separators;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} preparing and operating air compressors;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} preparing and starting steering gear and conducting
    appropriate tests to ensure the correct operation of the steering
    gear;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} testing boiler water-level gauges under normal
    working conditions;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} operating boilers, including the combustion system;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} transferring ballast and fresh water;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} lubricating machinery;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} pumping bilges;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} taking machinery readings and compiling the data in
    the engine-room log books; and

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} acting as assistant to the engineer in charge of
    the engineering watch.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship
  certificate who holds a First-class, Second-class, Third-class or
  Fourth-class Engineer, Steamship certificate shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} after obtaining a Fourth-class Engineer, Steamship
    certificate, acquire at least 6 months of service as an engineer on
    one or more motor vessels that have a propulsive power of at least
    500 kW, other than stationary MOUs; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pass a written and an oral examination to determine
    whether they have the engineering knowledge required by subparagraph
    4(c)(i) of the table to subsection (1).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a Fourth-class Engineer, Steamship
  certificate who holds a First-class, Second-class, Third-class or
  Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship certificate shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} after obtaining a Fourth-class Engineer, Motor Ship
    certificate, acquire at least 6 months of service as an engineer on
    one or more steamships that have a propulsive power of at least
    500 kW; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pass a written and an oral examination to determine
    whether they have the engineering knowledge required by subparagraph
    4(c)(ii) of the table to subsection (1).

#### [Chief Engineer, Motor Ship and Steamship Endorsements]{.HTitleText3} {#h-729997 .Subheading}

**[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Chief Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship endorsement shall meet the
requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this section and the
corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="729999">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The following certificates issued after
January 2, 1994 or received in exchange as indicated in paragraph 45(a)
of Schedule 1 to this Part:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Third-class Engineer, Motor
Ship certificate, in the case of an applicant for a Motor Ship
endorsement; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Third-class Engineer,
Steamship certificate, in the case of an applicant for a Steamship
endorsement.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>At least 24 months of sea service as an
engineer performing engine-room duties on one or more vessels that have
a propulsive power of at least 750 kW and are</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> motor vessels, in the case of
an applicant for a Motor Ship endorsement; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> steamships, in the case of an
applicant for a Steamship endorsement.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Certificate to be provided to the
examiner</td>
<td>Ship management practices taught using a propulsive plant
simulator.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Second Engineer, Motor Ship and Steamship Endorsement]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730005 .Subheading}

**[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Second Engineer, Motor Ship or Steamship endorsement shall meet the
requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this section and the
corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="730007">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Fourth-class Engineer, Motor
Ship certificate with STCW endorsement, in the case of an applicant for
a Motor Ship endorsement; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Fourth-class Engineer,
Steamship certificate with STCW endorsement, in the case of an applicant
for a Steamship endorsement.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Experience</td>
<td><p>At least 12 months of sea service as an engineer performing
engine-room duties on one or more vessels that have a propulsive power
of at least 750 kW and are</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> motor vessels, in the case of
an applicant for a Motor Ship endorsement; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> steamships, in the case of an
applicant for a Steamship endorsement.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Watchkeeping Engineer, Motor-driven Fishing Vessel]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730013 .Subheading}

**[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Watchkeeping Engineer, Motor-driven Fishing Vessel certificate shall
meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this section
and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="730015">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The following amount of sea service on one
or more motor vessels that have a propulsive power of at least 125 kW as
an engineer, engine-room rating or assistant engineer:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 12 months;
or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> at least 3 months if the
applicant has successfully completed an approved training program on
diesel engines.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
the examiner</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
basic safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
boats;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> ship watchkeeping practices
taught using a propulsive plant simulator; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> marine basic first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
<td><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Examination on ship
watchkeeping practices using a propulsive plant simulator, after
providing the certificate referred to in paragraph 2(c);</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> the following written
examinations related to motor-driven fishing vessels, after meeting the
requirements of items 1 and 2 and paragraph (a):</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> general engineering
knowledge, and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> engineering knowledge of
motor vessels; and</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> oral examination on each of
the subjects referred to in paragraph (b), after passing the
examinations referred to in that paragraph.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Small Vessel Machinery Operator]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730021 .Subheading}

- **[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Small Vessel Machinery Operator certificate shall meet
  the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection
  and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730024">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Acquire at least two months of qualifying
  service as follows:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 1 month of sea
  service as an engineer or a rating performing engine-room duties on one
  or more motor vessels; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> any remaining time any
  combination of the types of service set out in item 2 of the table to
  subsection 147(1).</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to small
  passenger vessel safety; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> marine basic first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Written examination on
  general engineering knowledge of small vessels, after meeting the
  requirements of items 1 and 2; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> after passing the examination
  referred to in paragraph (a),</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> oral examination on general
  engineering knowledge of small vessels if applying for an unrestricted
  certificate, and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> practical examination on
  board the vessel in respect of which the certificate is sought if
  applying for the restricted certificate referred to in
  subsection (2).</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite item 1 of the table to subsection (1), an
  applicant for a Small Vessel Machinery Operator certificate restricted
  for use on board a specified passenger-carrying vessel that has a
  propulsive power of less than 750 kW and is engaged on a limited near
  coastal voyage, Class 2 or a sheltered waters voyage may, instead of
  meeting the requirements of that item,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} successfully complete training related to the
    propulsion system and safety systems fitted on the vessel; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquire at least 10 days of sea service performing
    engine-room duties on the vessel or a vessel of the same class.

#### [Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class I]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730033 .Subheading}

- **[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for an Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class I
  certificate shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the
  table to this subsection and the corresponding specifications set out
  in column 2.

  <figure id="730036">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate or a
  licence</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Fourth-class Engineer, Motor
  Ship;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Aircraft Maintenance Engineer
  Licence issued under the <a href="/eng/acts/A-2">Aeronautics Act</a>;
  or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV)
  Engineer, Class II, if the applicant has acquired at least 24 months of
  service related to the maintenance of ACVs while holding that
  certificate.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates and other
  documents to be provided to the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Training course for engineers
  provided by a manufacturer of an ACV or by an agent of the authorized
  representative of an ACV if the agent has been trained by the
  manufacturer;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> whichever of the following
  periods of service is the longest, acquired as an engineer performing
  maintenance on one or more ACVs:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> 12 months of service,
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> the duration of one complete
  maintenance and verification cycle as recommended by the
  manufacturer;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> MED with respect to basic
  safety; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> marine basic first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>
  <td>Written examination on general knowledge and maintenance of air
  cushion vessels at the level of Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer,
  Class I, after meeting the other requirements of this table.</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for renewal of an Air Cushion Vehicle
  (ACV) Engineer, Class I certificate shall meet the requirements set
  out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and the corresponding
  specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730043">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Provide to the examiner a testimonial of
  service as ACV engineer for at least</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> 12 months in the 5 years
  before the application date for renewal of the certificate; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> 3 months in the 12 months
  before the application date for renewal of the certificate.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>
  <td>Written examination on general knowledge and maintenance of air
  cushion vessels at the level of Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer,
  Class I, if the applicant has not provided the testimonial set out in
  item 1.</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

#### [Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class II]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730049 .Subheading}

- **[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for an Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class II
  certificate shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the
  table to this subsection and the corresponding specifications set out
  in column 2.

  <figure id="730052">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> Aircraft Maintenance Engineer

  manufacturer;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> whichever of the following
  periods of service is the longest, acquired as a training engineer

  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>

  </tr>

  </table>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for renewal of an Air Cushion Vehicle

### [Specialized Certificates and Endorsements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-730065 .Subheading}

**[[154]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Proficiency in Fast Rescue Boats certificate or endorsement shall meet
the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this section and
the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="730068">

class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

**[[155]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Proficiency in Survival Craft and Rescue Boats Other Than Fast Rescue
Boats certificate or endorsement shall meet the requirements set out in
column 1 of the table to this section and the corresponding
specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="730076">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

Restricted Proficiency in Survival Craft and Rescue Boats Other Than

#### [Passenger Safety Management]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730090 .Subheading}

**[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Passenger Safety Management certificate or endorsement shall meet the
requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this section and the
corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="730092">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Certificates to be provided to the
examiner</td>
<td><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Passenger safety management
training certificate; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> marine basic first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Oil and Chemical Tanker Familiarization]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730106 .Subheading}

- **[[159]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for an Oil and Chemical Tanker Familiarization certificate
  or endorsement shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the
  table to this subsection and the corresponding specifications set out
  in column 2.

  <figure id="730109">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">If an applicant has not, within the 5 years
  before the application date for issuance of the certificate or
  endorsement, successfully completed an approved training course in oil
  and chemical tanker familiarization, at least 3 months of sea service
  within that period, on one or more oil or chemical tankers, performing
  duties relating to the loading, discharging or transferring of cargo and
  the operation of cargo equipment.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Certificates to be provided to the
  examiner</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
  basic safety;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> marine basic first aid;
  and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> unless the applicant has
  acquired the experience set out in item 1, an oil and chemical tanker
  familiarization training certificate obtained within the 5 years before
  the application date for issuance of the certificate or
  endorsement.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for renewal of an Oil and Chemical
  Tanker Familiarization endorsement shall meet the requirements set out
  in column 1 of the table to this subsection and the corresponding
  specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730116">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>If the applicant has not obtained the
  training certificate referred to in item 2, acquire the following
  service within the 5 years before the application date for renewal:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 3 months of service
  on one or more oil or chemical tankers performing duties relating to the
  loading, discharging or transferring of cargo and the operation of cargo
  equipment; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> at least 24 months as</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> a shore captain or marine
  superintendent in charge of supervising the loading or unloading of oil
  or chemical tankers, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> an instructor at a
  recognized institution having taught at least two approved training
  courses in oil and chemical tanker familiarization.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Certificate to be provided to the
  examiner</td>
  <td>If the applicant has not acquired the experience set out in item 1,
  an oil and chemical tanker familiarization training certificate obtained
  within the 5 years before the application date for renewal.</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

#### [Supervisor of an Oil Transfer Operation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730138 .Subheading}

**[[161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Supervisor of an Oil Transfer Operation certificate shall meet the
requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this section and the
corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="730140">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">At least 3 months of qualifying service
performing duties relating to oil transfer operations involving one or
more oil tankers or other vessels carrying oil for cargo.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Certificates to be provided to the
examiner</td>

<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to basic
safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> training with respect to
supervision of an oil transfer operation; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> marine basic first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Supervisor of an Oil Transfer Operation in Arctic Waters (North of 60° N)]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730146 .Subheading}

**[[162]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Supervisor of an Oil Transfer Operation in Arctic Waters (North of
60° N) certificate shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of
the table to this section and the corresponding specifications set out
in column 2.

<figure id="730148">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Within the 5 years before the application
date for issuance of the certificate, under the supervision of the
holder of a Supervisor of an Oil Transfer Operation in Arctic Waters
(North of 60° N) certificate,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> have assisted, while holding
a Supervisor of an Oil Transfer Operation certificate, in at least three
oil transfer operations in waters north of 60° N;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> have acquired two Arctic
shipping seasons of service, during which the applicant has assisted in
at least three oil transfer operations in waters north of 60° N;
or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> have assisted in at least 6
oil transfer operations in waters north of 60° N.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Certificates to be provided to the
examiner</td>
<td><p>If the applicant does not hold a Supervisor of an Oil Transfer
Operation certificate,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to basic
safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> training with respect to
supervision of an oil transfer operation; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> marine basic first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Specialized Oil Tanker Training]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730170 .Subheading}

- **[[165]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-165 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Specialized Oil Tanker Training endorsement shall meet
  the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection
  and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730173">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate and an
  endorsement</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>One of the following certificates with an
  Oil and Chemical Tanker Familiarization endorsement:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> any Master
  certificate;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> any Mate certificate;
  or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> any Engineer
  certificate.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">While holding an Oil and Chemical Tanker
  Familiarization endorsement, have acquired, within the 5 years before
  the application date for issuance of the endorsement, at least 3 months
  of sea service on one or more oil or chemical tankers as mate or
  engineer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Certificates and other documents to be
  provided to the examiner</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Marine advanced first
  aid;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> specialized oil tanker safety
  training certificate obtained within the 5 years before the application
  date for issuance of the endorsement; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> supplementary testimonial
  indicating that the applicant has acquired service assisting in cargo
  operations.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

  Training endorsement shall meet the requirements set out in column 1
  of the table to this subsection and the corresponding specifications
  set out in column 2.

  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 3 months on board

  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>

  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>

  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>

#### [Specialized Chemical Tanker Training]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730186 .Subheading}

- **[[166]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-166 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Specialized Chemical Tanker Training endorsement shall
  meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this
  subsection and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730189">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate and an
  endorsement</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>One of the following certificates with an
  Oil and Chemical Tanker Familiarization endorsement:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> any Master
  certificate;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> any Mate certificate;
  or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> any Engineer
  certificate.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">While holding an Oil and Chemical Tanker
  Familiarization endorsement, have acquired, within the 5 years before
  the application date for issuance of the endorsement, at least 3 months
  of sea service on one or more chemical tankers as a mate or an
  engineer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Certificates and other documents to be
  provided to the examiner</td>
  <td><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Marine advanced first
  aid;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> specialized chemical tanker
  safety training, obtained within the 5 years before the application date
  for issuance of the endorsement; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> supplementary testimonial
  indicating that the applicant has acquired service assisting in cargo
  operations.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for renewal of a Specialized Chemical
  Tanker Training endorsement shall meet the requirements set out in
  column 1 of the table to this subsection and the corresponding
  specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730196">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>If the applicant has not obtained the
  training certificate referred to in item 2, the following service within
  the 5 years before the application date for renewal:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 3 months on board
  one or more chemical tankers performing duties relating to the loading,
  discharging or transferring of cargo and the operation of cargo
  equipment; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> at least 24 months as</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> a shore captain or marine
  superintendent in charge of supervising the loading or unloading of
  chemical tankers, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> an instructor at a
  recognized institution having taught at least two approved training
  courses in specialized chemical tanker safety.</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Certificate to be provided to the
  examiner</td>
  <td>If the applicant has not acquired the experience set out in item 1,
  a specialized chemical tanker safety training certificate obtained
  within the 5 years before the application date for renewal.</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

  applicant for a Specialized Liquefied Gas Tanker Training endorsement

#### [High-Speed Craft (HSC) Type Rating]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730218 .Subheading}

- **[[168]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a High-Speed Craft (HSC) Type Rating certificate shall
  meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this
  subsection and the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730221">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">A Master, a Mate or an Engineer certificate
  valid for the craft and the route as set out in Part 2.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Testimonial to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">Training testimonial indicating that the
  applicant has received the type rating training set out in
  sections 18.3.3.1 to 18.3.3.12 of the HSC Code.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>
  <td>The examination set out in section 18.3.3 of the HSC Code, after
  meeting the other requirements of this table.</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for renewal of a High-Speed Craft (HSC)
  Type Rating certificate shall meet the requirements set out in
  column 1 of the table to this subsection and the corresponding
  specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730228">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Testimonial of service as a master, a mate
  or an engineer having an operational role on the high-speed craft or one
  or more sister vessels having the same type of route as the route in
  respect of which the renewal is sought, for at least</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> 6 months within the 24 months
  before the application date for renewal; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> 2 months within the 12 months
  before the application date for renewal.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>
  <td>The examination set out in section 18.3.3 of the HSC Code if the
  applicant has not acquired the experience set out in item 1.</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>

#### [Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Type Rating]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730234 .Subheading}

- **[[169]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-169 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  master or mate applying for an Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Type Rating
  certificate shall meet the requirements set out in column 1 of the
  table to this subsection and the corresponding specifications set out
  in column 2.

  <figure id="730237">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">A Master or Mate certificate valid for the
  ACV and the route as specified in Part 2.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> To obtain a restricted
  certificate valid for use on board an ACV only between sunrise and
  sunset, in good visibility and in non-icing conditions,</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> in the case of an ACV of more
  than 10 000 kg all up weight, at least 100 hours of training in control
  of one or more ACVs of the same type as the ACV in respect of which the
  certificate is sought, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> in the case of an ACV of
  10 000 kg or less all up weight, at least 25 hours of training in
  control of one or more ACVs of the same type as the ACV in respect of
  which the certificate is sought;</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> to obtain a certificate valid
  for use on board an ACV between sunset and sunrise and in limited
  visibility, the experience required by paragraph (a) and at least
  50 hours of radar pilotage; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> to obtain a certificate valid
  for use on board an ACV in icing conditions, the experience required by
  paragraph (a) and at least 25 hours of training in control of one or
  more ACVs while in icing conditions.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Testimonial to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">Training testimonial with respect to a
  training course for the person in command given by the manufacturer of
  the ACV in respect of which the certificate is sought or by an agent of
  the authorized representative of the ACV if that agent has been trained
  by the manufacturer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
  <td><p>After meeting the other requirements of this table,</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> a written examination on
  general knowledge of air cushion vessels; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> the examination set out in
  section 18.3.3 of the HSC Code.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A master or mate applying for renewal of an Air
  Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Type Rating certificate shall meet the
  requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and
  the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730244">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Testimonial of service as a master or mate
  on one or more ACVs of the same type as the ACV in respect of which the
  renewal is sought, for at least</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> 100 hours within the
  24 months before the application date for renewal; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> 25 hours of training in the
  12 months before the application date for renewal.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>
  <td>The examination set out in section 18.3.3 of the HSC Code if the
  applicant has not acquired the experience set out in item 1.</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An engineer applying for an Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV)
  Type Rating certificate shall meet the requirements set out in
  column 1 of the table to this subsection and the corresponding
  specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730251">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">Air Cushion Vehicle (ACV) Engineer, Class I
  or Class II.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Testimonial of the following service as
  assistant engineer on one or more ACVs of the same type as the ACV in
  respect of which the certificate is sought:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> in the case of an ACV of more
  than 10 000 kg all up weight, the maintenance required in respect of at
  least 100 operating hours; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in the case of an ACV of up
  to 10 000 kg all up weight, the maintenance required in respect of at
  least 25 operating hours.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Testimonial to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">Training testimonial with respect to a
  training course for engineers given by the manufacturer of the ACV to
  which the certificate of competency relates or by an agent of the
  authorized representative of the ACV if that agent has been trained by
  the manufacturer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>
  <td>Oral examination or, if available, a written examination, followed
  by a practical examination regarding the type of ACV in respect of which
  the certificate is sought, after meeting the other requirements of this
  table.</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} An engineer applying for renewal of an Air Cushion
  Vehicle (ACV) Type Rating certificate shall meet the requirements set
  out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and the corresponding
  specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730258">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>

  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"

  engineer on one or more ACVs of the same type as the ACV in respect of

  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> the maintenance required in

  </ul></td>

  <td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>

### [Ratings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-730264 .Subheading}

#### [Able Seafarer]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730265 .Subheading}

**[[170]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-170 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for an
Able Seafarer certificate shall meet the requirements set out in
column 1 of the table to this section and the corresponding
specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="730267">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">At least 36 months of sea service on one or
more vessels as a rating performing deck duties.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates and other
documents to be provided to the examiner</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
basic safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
boats;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> steering testimonial
attesting to the applicant’s ability to steer and containing the
declaration and at least the information set out in Schedule 3 to this
Part; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> marine basic first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
<td><p>The following examinations on general seamanship, after meeting
the other requirements of this table:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> a written examination;
and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> a practical
examination.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Bridge Watch Rating]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730273 .Subheading}

**[[171]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Bridge Watch Rating certificate shall meet the requirements set out in
column 1 of the table to this section and the corresponding
specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="730275">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The following sea service performing
bridge watchkeeping duties on one or more vessels:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> at least 6 months;
or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> at least 2 months if the
applicant has successfully completed an approved training program in
bridge watchkeeping duties.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates and other document
to be provided to the examiner</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
basic safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
boats;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> steering testimonial
attesting to the applicant’s ability to steer and containing the
declaration and at least the information set out in Schedule 3 to this
Part; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> marine basic first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>
<td>Written examination on general seamanship, after meeting the other
requirements of this table.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Engine-room Rating]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730281 .Subheading}

**[[172]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-172 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for an
Engine-room Rating certificate shall meet the requirements set out in
column 1 of the table to this section and the corresponding
specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="730283">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> At least 6 months of
qualifying service consisting of</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> at least 3 months of sea
service as an engine-room rating, and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> any remaining time in any
combination of the type of service required for a Fourth-class Engineer
certificate, as set out in item 2 of the table to subsection 147(1);
or</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> if the applicant has obtained
a training certificate in engine-room rating duties, at least 3 months
of qualifying service consisting of</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> at least 2 months of sea
service as an engine-room rating, and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> any remaining time in any
combination of the type of service required for a Fourth-class Engineer
certificate, as set out in item 2 of the table to
subsection 147(1).</p></li>
</ul></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
the examiner</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
basic safety;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> MED training with respect to
proficiency in survival craft and rescue boats other than fast rescue
boats; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> marine basic first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>
<td><p>One of the following examinations on the duties of an engine-room
rating, after meeting the other requirements of this table:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> a written examination;
or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> an oral examination.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Ship's Cook]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730289 .Subheading}

**[[173]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Ship's Cook certificate shall meet the requirements set out in column 1
of the table to this section and the corresponding specifications set
out in column 2.

<figure id="730291">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Acquire at least 1 month of qualifying
service on one or more vessels as a ship’s cook or cook’s helper.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
the examiner</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> MED with respect to STCW
basic safety; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> marine advanced first
aid.</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass examinations</td>
<td><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Written examination on the
duties of a ship’s cook; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> practical examination on the
duties of a ship’s cook unless the applicant provides the examiner
with</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> a cook certificate recognized
by a province, or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> a training certificate
indicating that the applicant has successfully completed a cooking
course.</p></li>
</ul></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

#### [Compass Adjuster]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730297 .Subheading}

**[[174]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174 .sectionLabel}** An applicant for a
Compass Adjuster certificate shall meet the requirements set out in
column 1 of the table to this section and the corresponding
specifications set out in column 2.

<figure id="730299">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>

<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Requirements</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Experience</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Within three years before the application
date for issuance of the certificate, have completed 12 compensations or
adjustments of different magnetic compasses on board more than one
vessel, of which no fewer than 4 are on board steel vessels.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Pass an examination</td>
<td>Magnetic compass adjustment.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</figure>

#### [Sailing Vessel Endorsements]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730305 .Subheading}

- **[[175]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a sailing vessel endorsement shall meet the requirements
  set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and the
  corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730308">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> Any Master certificate;
  or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> any Mate
  certificate.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Certificate to be provided to the
  examiner</td>
  <td>Training certificate in sailing applicable to the type of rig.</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to meeting the requirements set out in
  subsection (1), an applicant for a sailing vessel endorsement listed
  in column 1 of the table to this subsection shall acquire the sea
  service requirements set out in column 2 on one or more sail training
  vessels, or any other sailing vessels, of the same type of rig in
  respect of which the endorsement is sought.

  <figure id="730315">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Endorsement
  Sought</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Fore and Aft Sailing Vessel,
  Unlimited</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> At least 12 months, including
  at least 6 months while the vessels are engaged on voyages other than
  sheltered waters voyages; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> at least 6 months from an
  approved program of on-board training, including at least 3 months while
  the vessels are engaged on voyages other than sheltered waters
  voyages.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Fore and Aft Sailing Vessel,
  Seasonal (April 15 to November 1)</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">At least 1 month performing deck duties.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Square Rig Sailing Vessel,
  Unlimited</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> At least 18 months, including
  at least 6 months while the vessels are engaged on voyages other than
  sheltered waters voyages; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> at least 9 months, from an
  approved program of on-board training, including at least 3 months while
  the vessels are engaged on voyages other than sheltered waters
  voyages.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Square Rig Sailing Vessel, Seasonal (April 15
  to November 1)</td>
  <td>At least 2 months performing deck duties.</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

#### [Passenger Submersible Craft Endorsement]{.HTitleText3} {#h-730321 .Subheading}

- **[[176]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-176 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a Passenger Submersible Craft endorsement shall meet the
  requirements set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and
  the corresponding specifications set out in column 2.

  <figure id="730324">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Requirements</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specifications</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hold a certificate</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">A Master, a Mate or an Engineer certificate
  valid for the vessel and the route as specified in Part 2.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Certificates to be provided to
  the examiner</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> A commercial diver
  certificate recognized by a province; and</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> marine basic first
  aid.</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Testimonial to be provided to the
  examiner</td>
  <td>Training testimonial with respect to training provided by the
  manufacturer of the vessel in respect of which the endorsement is sought
  or by an agent of the authorized representative of the vessel if the
  agent has been trained by the manufacturer.</td>
  </tr>

  out in column 2.
